You are the second-opinion reviewer for **Glyphbound**, a first-person RPG shooter in Unreal Engine 5.9.0 (C++), made for a 4-day game jam. Do not modify any file. Do not build or run anything.

## Context
- Read `Design/ARCHITECTURE.md` first (short). The design of this change is in `Design/GDD.md`, section 18, the "Reload" bullet and its two sub-bullets about the shotgun.
- The implementer's brief is `Prompts/feedback15_shotgun_viewmodel_pump_shells.md`: it is the specification of what was asked.
- The change is the uncommitted work in these files (compare with `git diff HEAD -- <file>`): `Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/Weapons/GBGun.h`, `Weapons/GBGun.cpp`, `Weapons/GBHeldItem.h`, `Weapons/GBEquipmentComponent.cpp`, `Core/GBStatNames.h`, `UI/GBHUD.cpp`, `Tests/GBWeaponsTests.cpp`, `Glyphbound/Content/Data/Weapons.json`, and the shotgun block of `Core/GBDevCommands.cpp`.

- Engine source for checking APIs: `C:\Program Files\Epic Games\UE_5.9\Engine\Source`.

## What to look for (real defects only, in this priority order)
1. **Ammo and reload state.** The shotgun reloads one round at a time and keeps its reserve in rounds inside `FGBAmmoState::SpareMagazines`. Look for: any reader or writer of `SpareMagazines` anywhere in the code that still treats the shotgun's value as magazines (pickups, inventory, equipment's full ammo state, stored guns, the HUD, era travel and respawn refills, dev commands); rounds created or lost when a reload is stopped by firing, by a weapon switch, by `SetAmmo` or `RefillAmmo`, or when a frame is longer than one shell's cycle; a reload that can start or continue with a full gun or an empty reserve; fire queued during a reload firing twice or never; division by zero when `MagazineSize` or `ReloadTime` is 0 or missing.
2. **Regressions on the pistol and the rifle.** They are approved by the owner and must behave exactly as before: reload timing, rounds lost on an early reload, the `--` display, the reload animation and its dev pin, the shot kick, the trigger, the glow, the energy bar and its blink.
3. **The pump and the reload look.** State left behind after a weapon switch, a death or an era travel (pump off its rest place, shell visible, gun stuck in its reload pose); two places writing the viewmodel root's transform; the dev pins changing gameplay state.
4. **The pure reload logic and its tests** (`Tests/GBWeaponsTests.cpp`): for each new test, name a wrong implementation that would still pass it, if there is one.
5. **Packaged build:** editor-only APIs outside guards; meshes loaded by path at runtime that the cook would not include (see `Glyphbound/Config/DefaultGame.ini` and how the pistol's and rifle's meshes are cooked).
6. **Gameplay numbers hardcoded** where the data should decide (visual offsets and animation shares in code are allowed by the project's rules).

Do not report: style, naming, formatting, missing comments, micro-optimizations, hypothetical multiplayer issues (the game is single player), or anything about the art values being provisional.

Finish with one short paragraph: what you would fix first, and what you checked and found solid.

# Glyphbound: code architecture

> Written by the architect (Claude Code) on 2026-11-12. Source of truth for how the C++ is organized.
> Design lives in `Design/GDD.md`; this file says how the code implements it. Keep it short and current.

## Principles
1. **Data, not code.** Every gameplay number lives in `Glyphbound/Content/Data/*.json` and is read through `UGBDataSubsystem`. Code never hardcodes damage, costs, speeds, durations, sizes or timings. The JSON ships as loose files, so numbers can be tuned in a packaged build with a text editor (`gb.ReloadData` reloads while playing).
2. **One stat system.** A number is a *named stat*: base value from data, then modifiers. `UGBStatsComponent` evaluates `(Base + adds) * multipliers` (or an override). Skill-tree nodes, armor, status effects, air passives and the weapon enchant are all just groups of modifiers with a source name. Nothing else changes numbers.
3. **One damage path.** Everything that hurts goes through `UGBCombatLibrary::DealDamage(Target, FGBDamageInfo)`. Shields, resistances, teams, on-hit effects and kill credit hook in there once.
4. **Spells are data + a behavior.** A spell is a `FGBSpellDefinition` (params with `Base/Min/Max/ScaledBy`) plus the class of an `AGBSpellActor` behavior. The QTE result (power, reach) scales the params tagged for it. A new spell that fits an existing behavior is a JSON entry, no code.
5. **Shared pieces.** One projectile (`AGBProjectile`) for staff bolts, spell projectiles, enemy shots and grenades. One status-effect component for slows, burns, potions and buffs. One interact interface for portals, chests, pickups and the seal.
6. **Text in git.** No binary assets are required to run: input actions, the test arena, placeholder shapes and placeholder pixel sprites are all built in C++ from text. `Content/` is ignored by git except `Content/Data/`.
7. **No GAS.** Unreal's Gameplay Ability System was considered and rejected for the jam: it needs a lot of boilerplate and asset setup, and the game's needs (costs, cooldowns, timed effects, stat upgrades) are covered by principles 2-4 in a few hundred lines we fully control.
8. **PIE is human-only.** Agents build, run the pure-logic tests and package; a person plays.

## Module layout (`Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound`, one runtime module, prefix `GB`)
Includes are relative to the module root: `#include "Stats/GBStatsComponent.h"`.

| Folder | What | Key types |
|---|---|---|
| `Core/` | Shared enums, stat names, placeholders (plain shapes and pixel-textured structures), game mode, dev commands | `GBTypes.h`, `GBStatNames.h`, `GBPlaceholder.h` (`SetupTexturedShape`), `AGBGameMode`, `UGBGameInstance`, `GBDevCommands.cpp` (`gb.Smoke`) |
| `Data/` | Definitions and the JSON loader | `GBDataTypes.h`, `UGBDataSubsystem` |
| `Stats/` | Stats + modifiers, health/mana | `UGBStatsComponent`, `UGBAttributeComponent` |
| `Combat/` | Damage (one path, one global `OnDamageDealt` event), status effects, elemental build-up, projectile, explosion shockwave | `UGBCombatLibrary`, `UGBStatusEffectComponent`, `UGBElementalStatusComponent`, `FGBBuildUpMeter` (pure, tested), `AGBProjectile`, `AGBExplosionEffect` |
| `Player/` | Character, controller, input | `AGBPlayerCharacter`, `AGBPlayerController`, `UGBInputSet` |
| `Weapons/` | Held items and slots | `UGBEquipmentComponent`, `AGBHeldItem`, `AGBStaff`, `AGBGun`, `AGBSword` |
| `Spells/` | Spellbook, cast resolution, behaviors | `UGBSpellbookComponent`, `FGBSpellCast`, `AGBSpellActor`, `AGBSpell_*` |
| `QTE/` | Quick-time event | `FGBQTESession`, `FGBGestureRecognizer`, `UGBQTEComponent` |
| `World/` | Eras, progress, interactables, test arena | `UGBEraSubsystem`, `UGBEraComponent`, `UGBProgressSubsystem`, `IGBInteractable`, `AGBPortal`, `AGBChest`, `AGBPickup`, `AGBCheckpointVolume`, `AGBTestArena` |
| `AI/` | Enemies (navmesh path, local steering fallback, stuck check) and encounters | `AGBEnemyCharacter`, `GBSteering` (pure decision, tested), `AGBEncounter` |
| `Items/` | Inventory, consumables, armor, recipes | `UGBInventoryComponent` |
| `Progression/` | Skill trees | `UGBSkillTreeComponent` |
| `UI/` | Canvas HUD (two skins), menus, pixel sprites | `AGBHUD`, `UGBPixelSpriteLibrary` |
| `Tests/` | Pure-logic automation tests | `Glyphbound.<Area>.<Name>` |

## Data files (`Glyphbound/Content/Data`)
`Player.json`, `QTE.json`, `Game.json`, `Spells.json`, `Weapons.json`, `StatusEffects.json`, `Items.json` (items + recipes), `Enemies.json`, `Encounters.json`, `Skills.json`, `Sprites.json`. Schemas are the structs in `Data/GBDataTypes.h` (field name = JSON key). Stat and parameter names are the constants in `Core/GBStatNames.h`.

Modifier keys: an actor stat (`MoveSpeed`), `Weapon.<Id>.<Stat>`, `Weapon.<Slot>.<Stat>` (`Weapon.Staff`, `Weapon.Gun`, `Weapon.Sword`), `Weapon.All.<Stat>`, `Spell.<Id>.<Param>`, `Spell.All.<Param>`, `QTE.TimeDilation`...

Standard status effect ids (defined in `StatusEffects.json`, referenced from other files): `Slow`, `Ignite`, `HealthPotion`, `ManaPotion`, `HealthInjection`, `Enchant_Fire`, `Enchant_Lightning`, `Enchant_Ice`. (`Burn`, `Shock`, `Chill` were removed in GDD v0.22.)

Modifier ops: `Add`, `Multiply`, `Override`, and `MultiplyMin` (only the lowest one on a stat counts: slows never stack, the strongest wins).

Elemental keys (GDD 6 "Elemental build-up"): per enemy in `Enemies.json` `Stats` (`<E>DamageTaken`, `<E>BuildUpTaken`, `<E>BuildUpThreshold`, `<E>BuildUpDecayDelay`, `<E>BuildUpDecayRate`, `<E>StatusDuration`, `<E>StatusImmunity`, `IgniteDamageMult`, `ShockDischargeDamage`, `FrostSlow`; E = Fire, Lightning, Ice) and `Strings` (`<E>StatusReaction`); missing keys fall back to `Game.json` `ElementalDefault.<Key>`. Sources: spell param `BuildUp`, weapon stats `FireBuildUp` / `LightningBuildUp` / `IceBuildUp` and `WeakPointBuildUpMult`, item stat `BuildUp`.

## How the main flows run
- **Cast:** input -> `UGBSpellbookComponent::TryCastSlot` (checks staff in hand, known, cooldown, mana; spends mana) -> `UGBQTEComponent::StartQTE` (slow time, sequence = power, gesture = reach; or instant when off) -> `GBSpell::ResolveCast` (data -> QTE scaling -> modifiers) -> spawn the behavior actor -> it deals damage through `UGBCombatLibrary`.
- **Shoot:** input -> `AGBGun` (fire interval, magazine, spread, recoil, pellets; every number via `GetStat`) -> hitscan -> headshot and distance falloff math in `UGBCombatLibrary` -> `DealDamage`.
- **Era travel:** `AGBPortal::Interact` -> `UGBEraSubsystem::RequestTravel` (fade out, swap era for `UGBEraComponent` actors and `_Past` / `_Future` sub-levels, teleport, restore health/mana/ammo, save checkpoint, fade in).
- **Progress:** encounters set flags in `UGBProgressSubsystem` when cleared; `UGBEraComponent` reads flags, which is how era-gated zones and past-to-future unlocks are built. Death restores the last checkpoint snapshot; flags are never rolled back (enemies do not respawn).
- **Elemental build-up:** a hit carries `FGBDamageInfo::BuildUp` (spell param, weapon stat by element, item stat) and a `BuildUpEventId` (one per shot, cast, blast or tick). `DealDamage` passes it to the target's `UGBElementalStatusComponent` (`ApplyBuildUp`; the ice floor, which deals no damage, calls it directly), which dedupes by event id, applies the enemy's resistance and fills one of three `FGBBuildUpMeter`s. A full bar starts the status: Ignite is the `Ignite` status effect (DOT through `DealDamage`); Shock deals its discharge; Frost slow is a `MultiplyMin` modifier; stun, interrupt and freeze are done by `AGBEnemyCharacter` from the component's `OnStatusStarted` event. The component only ticks (every `ElementalTickInterval`) while a bar is not empty. Looks bind to its events; `gb.ShowElemental 1` is a debug view and `gb.BuildUp` a test command.
- **Upgrades:** `UGBSkillTreeComponent::Unlock` adds the node's modifiers to the player's stats; weapons and spells pick them up on their next read.

## Conventions
- Game text in English. Code comments in English. ASCII only in source files.
- Native multicast delegates (C++ project; no Blueprint graphs needed).
- No custom collision channels: projectiles and queries use the default channels and filter by team in code.
- Pointers to definitions are not kept across frames (data can be reloaded): keep the id, look it up.
- Tests: only pure logic that is easy to break and hard to see by playing; each one must be able to fail; names `Glyphbound.<Area>.<Name>` where a name is never also a prefix of another.
- Build: `powershell -File Tools/Jam/Build-Jam.ps1` (Editor closed). One build at a time per machine.
- Art rule in code: characters, items and effects are camera-facing pixel sprites; **structures are 3D shapes with pixel tiles** (`GBPlaceholder::SetupTexturedShape`, tiles are sprites of `Sprites.json` named `tile_*`). The material behind it (`M_PixelSurface`) is the only binary asset and is generated by `Tools/Jam/Make-Materials.ps1`, never edited by hand.
- A structure that looks different in each era has ONE hidden collision body present in both eras and two non-colliding looks (PastOnly / FutureOnly), so footing and navigation never change on era travel.
- UI that reacts to combat listens to `UGBCombatLibrary::OnDamageDealt()`; nothing in combat code knows about the HUD.

# Task: the shotgun in the game: its 3D model, a pump after each shot, and a shell-by-shell reload

Project: **Glyphbound** (Unreal Engine 5.9.0, C++), in `Glyphbound/`. Read `Design/ARCHITECTURE.md` and `Prompts/implementer_rules.md` first. You DO build and run things in this task; the rest of "Hard rules" applies. Constraints:
- One build or run at a time. Other chats use this machine: if a build says another one holds the mutex, wait for it. If a build fails in files that are not yours, wait a few minutes and try again; if it still fails, stop and say so.
- Do not open the Unreal Editor and do not launch PIE. If a build fails with "Unable to build while Live Coding is active", someone has the Editor open: stop and say so in your report.
- Do not commit. Do not run `Tools/Jam/Import-*.ps1`, and do not touch `Content/` except `Content/Data/*.json`.
- Another chat has uncommitted work in this tree (the Entity: `AI/GBEntity*`, `AI/GBTentacle*`, `Combat/GBCombatLibrary.*`, `Combat/GBProjectile.*`, `Tests/GBEntityTests.cpp`, and **its own blocks inside `Core/GBDevCommands.cpp`**). Touch only the files listed under "Files you own"; in `GBDevCommands.cpp` add your shotgun block and change nothing else.
- The pistol and the rifle are approved by the owner: what they do and how they look must not change. Every existing smoke line for them must stay as it is.

Owner's decisions (Spanish, translated; `Design/GDD.md` v0.33, section 18 "Reload"):
- The shotgun gets the same treatment as the pistol and the rifle: a real 3D first-person model, complete and working.
- Reload: "Cartucho a cartucho" ("shell by shell").
- Pump: "SÃ­, con corredera" ("yes, with a pump"): the forend pumps after every shot.

## What exists today
- `Weapons/GBGun.h/.cpp`: the pistol and the rifle are built by one code path from a per-model art description (`FGBGunModelArt`, constants `PistolArt` and `RifleArt`, and the list in `FindModelArt`). `Definition.ViewModel` in `Content/Data/Weapons.json` picks the model. The shotgun (`"Id": "Shotgun"`) has no `ViewModel`, so it shows placeholder shapes.
- The reload is a magazine swap: `Reload()` starts a timer of `ReloadTime`, `FinishReload()` takes one spare magazine and fills the gun. `FGBAmmoState` (in `Weapons/GBHeldItem.h`) holds `InMagazine` and `SpareMagazines`.
- The reload animation (`GBReloadAnim`, `ApplyViewModelPose`, `ApplyReloadMagazine`, `SetReloadPreview`) is driven by `GetReloadAnimProgress()`; the HUD's 2D hands follow it (`UI/GBHUD.cpp`, `DrawHands`).
- The shotgun's art is imported: `/Game/Glyphbound/Weapons/Shotgun/` holds `SM_Shotgun_Body`, `SM_Shotgun_Trigger`, `SM_Shotgun_Magazine`, `SM_Shotgun_WeakPointLight`, `SM_Shotgun_AmmoDisplay` and a new part, `SM_Shotgun_Pump`. The materials take the same parameters as the rifle's (`ElementColor`, `ImbueRemaining`, `Flash`, `DisplayTexture`); the energy bar is inside the body's material (no separate mesh), 13 cells.
  - `SM_Shotgun_Magazine` is **one shell**, not a magazine. Its rest place is inside the loading port on the gun's underside, where the body hides it. It has a brass base at its rear end and a band that glows.
  - `SM_Shotgun_Pump` is a ribbed sleeve around the forend. It slides back along one vector and returns.
- Offsets, from `Glyphbound/SourceArt/Weapons/Shotgun/export_report.json`, in centimetres in the body's frame (X forward, Z up), same convention as the rifle's values:

| What | Value (cm) |
|---|---|
| Trigger hinge (`TriggerOffset`) | (5.8, 0, 0.6) |
| Shell at rest (`MagazineRest`) | (18.47, 0, -5.04) |
| Shell's way out of the port (`MagazineOut`) | (-5.0, 0, -1.81) |
| Pump at rest | (49.9, 0, -10.45) |
| Pump's slide to the back of its travel | (-4.11, 0, 0.88) |
| Muzzle tip (`MuzzleTip`) | (65.48, 0, -1.24) |
| Ammo display's centre (for reference; it is in the sight's rear window, a 2:1 pane 5 cm wide) | (18.85, 0.85, 20.45) |
| The gun is 101.2 cm long (the rifle is 100 cm and uses `ViewScale` 0.62) | |

## Part A: the model
- Add `ShotgunArt` and list it in `FindModelArt`; set `"ViewModel": "Shotgun"` on the shotgun in `Weapons.json`.
- The pump is a new optional part of a model: give `FGBGunModelArt` what it needs (whether the model has one, its rest offset, its slide vector). A model without a pump must behave exactly as today.
- Idle glow: the gun's own cyan, linear (0.10, 0.85, 1.00). `bDrawDisplayFrame` true (the pane is plain, like the rifle's): with 6 shells the display shows the count and 6 pips.
- Framing (`ViewScale`, `RootOffset`, `RootYaw`), kick and reload-pose values are yours to tune from the smoke screenshots. Start from the rifle's. Goals: the gun sits in the lower right like the rifle; the barrel's line runs to the crosshair; the sight's rear window with the ammo hologram and the energy bar just behind the sight (on top of the receiver) are both in view; nothing clips the near plane (3 cm) or covers the crosshair. This gun has a tall thumbhole stock that rises 15 cm above the grip: keep it from filling the lower right corner.
- The weak-point light is a lens on the sight's rear lower panel, under the window: it must flash on a head or weak-point hit like the other guns'.

## Part B: the pump
After every shot the pump slides back and returns, inside the time between shots. It is only the look: `FireInterval` and every gameplay timing stay as they are.
- Timing as shares of the gun's current `FireInterval` (so a faster fire rate plays it faster), in named constants next to the other viewmodel constants: provisional values start 0.22, fully back 0.45, home 0.72.
- While it moves, the gun gives a small rack motion through `ApplyViewModelPose` (the one place that writes the root's transform): a short dip and roll, per-model art values, provisional. It adds to the shot kick; it does not replace it.
- The HUD's left hand rides the pump: back and forward with it by a per-model amount in sprite pixels. The right hand stays as it is.
- No pump while a reload is running. A weapon switch resets it to rest at once.
- A dev-only pin like the reload's (`SetPumpPreview(float Amount)` / `ClearPumpPreview()`, or one shared mechanism) so the smoke can photograph the pump at the back of its travel.
- Accessors the smoke needs: whether the model has a pump, and how far the pump is from rest in cm.

## Part C: the shell-by-shell reload (gameplay)
`Design/GDD.md` section 18, "The shotgun loads shell by shell", is the specification. In short:
- A gun reloads per round when its data says so. Add a stat for it (name it in `Core/GBStatNames.h`, one line, e.g. `ReloadPerRound`; 1 on the shotgun in `Weapons.json`, absent = 0 on the others).
- **Reserve.** For such a gun `FGBAmmoState::SpareMagazines` counts spare **rounds** (write that in the comment on the field). Its maximum is the `SpareMagazines` stat times `MagazineSize` (4 x 6 = 24, the same total as before). `AddSpareMagazines(Count)` adds `Count` magazines' worth of rounds, clamped, and still returns how many magazines' worth it added, rounded up, so callers that test "did it add anything" keep working. `Weapons/GBEquipmentComponent.cpp` builds a full ammo state for a gun from its stats (around line 117): it must give 24, not 4, to a per-round gun. Find every reader of `SpareMagazines` (grep) and make sure none is left treating rounds as magazines.
- **Cycle.** One shell takes `ReloadTime / MagazineSize` (0.33 s; `ReloadTime` already carries the Rapid Reload perk). When a cycle ends one round moves from the reserve into the gun. If the gun is not full and the reserve is not empty, the next cycle starts at once; otherwise the reload ends. A full reload from empty therefore takes `ReloadTime`, as the balance tables assume. A frame longer than one cycle must not lose time or shells.
- **Stopping part-way.** Pressing fire during the reload stops it, and the gun shoots as soon as its normal fire rules allow. The shell on its way in is not lost: it stays in the reserve. With an empty gun, fire pressed during the reload waits for the first shell to land, then stops the reload and shoots. A weapon switch cancels the reload; shells already loaded stay loaded.
- Nothing is lost by reloading early (unlike the magazine guns). An empty shotgun still starts reloading by itself.
- `Reload()`, `FinishReload()` and the rest must behave exactly as today for the pistol and the rifle.
- `GetReloadProgress()` for a per-round reload: the whole reload's progress, 0 to 1 over the shells it set out to load.
- The ammo hologram shows the live count during a per-round reload (not `--`), and a pip lights as each shell goes in.
- HUD (`UI/GBHUD.cpp`, the ammo panel around line 980): for a per-round gun the reserve label reads `x%d shells`, not `x%d mags`. The HUD must not need to know the weapon's id: give `AGBHeldItem` a small virtual (default false) that the gun overrides.
- **Pure logic, with tests.** Put the stepping of a per-round reload (time in, rounds moved, when it ends) in a small plain struct or free functions with no world and no actor, and test it in `Tests/GBWeaponsTests.cpp` (rules in `implementer_rules.md`; a test name must not be a prefix of another). Tests that can each fail:
  - a full reload from empty moves exactly `MagazineSize` rounds and ends at `ReloadTime`, not a cycle early or late;
  - a reserve smaller than the empty space stops when the reserve is gone, with nothing negative;
  - one step longer than three cycles moves three rounds and keeps the remainder of the time;
  - stopping part-way keeps the rounds already moved and puts none back.

## Part D: the shell-by-shell reload (look)
The same pose mechanism as the magazine guns, but the middle repeats once per shell.
- The gun leaves rest for its reload pose during the first shell's first share, holds it while shells go in, and eases back to rest after the last shell (that last ease may run a little past the gameplay end of the reload, like a cancelled reload's blend; the gun must be able to fire when gameplay says so).
- The reload pose must show the loading port: it is on the underside, in front of the trigger guard, so the gun rolls and rises until its underside faces the camera.
- Per shell: the shell (the `Magazine` part) appears below the view, rises to the end of the `MagazineOut` path, slides in along it to rest, fastest at the end, and a short bump seats it. Then it is hidden until the next one. At rest, outside a reload, the shell is hidden (it is inside the port).
- The left hand follows each shell: down to fetch, up with it, the push. The right hand follows the gun as it does today.
- A stopped or cancelled reload must not snap: shell hidden at once, gun blends to rest in about 0.12 s, as today.
- The dev pin must be able to hold one shell's cycle still at a given progress for the smoke.
- Keep it one code path where that is natural: the per-shell cycle should reuse the existing beat helpers rather than copy them.

## Files you own

- `Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/Weapons/GBHeldItem.h` (the comment on `SpareMagazines`, the new virtual)
- `Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/Weapons/GBEquipmentComponent.cpp`: only the full ammo state of a gun
- `Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/Core/GBStatNames.h`: one new stat name
- `Glyphbound/Source/Glyphbound/UI/GBHUD.cpp`: `DrawHands` and the reserve label of the ammo panel only

- If another file reads `SpareMagazines` in a way that breaks for rounds (`Items/GBInventoryComponent.cpp`, `World/GBPickup.cpp`), say what you found; change it only if the fix is one or two lines, and list it.

## The smoke run
`gb.Smoke` has a block per gun in `Core/GBDevCommands.cpp` (search for `"rifle:"`). Add a shotgun block after the rifle's, in the same style, with screenshots `15a` onwards. Checks, each able to fail:
- the shotgun is in hand with its real model: 6 meshes (body, trigger, shell, weak-point light, ammo display, pump), not placeholders; the display shows `6`; screenshot `15a_shotgun_hip`;
- one shot: the display shows `5`, the kick and the muzzle flash happen, and the pump leaves rest and comes back to rest before the next shot is allowed; with the pump pinned at the back of its travel, screenshot `15b_shotgun_pump`;
- weak-point flash on a head hit, 0 on a body hit; the energy bar at half an enchant; screenshot `15c_shotgun_enchanted`;
- reload from 3 shells with 24 in reserve: after one cycle 4 and 23, the display follows; it ends at 6 and 21 after about half of `ReloadTime`; with a shell's cycle pinned mid-way (the shell visible and away from rest, the root away from rest), screenshot `15d_shotgun_reload_shell`;
- fire pressed during a reload from 2 shells: the reload stops, the shot is fired, and the counts add up (nothing lost, nothing created);
- a reload cancelled by switching to the staff and back keeps the shells already loaded;
- after all of it the pump and the shell are at rest and the root is at rest.
Every other smoke line must stay as it is.

## How to verify (from `C:\GameJam`)
Project rule (`CLAUDE.md`, "Tiempo de iteraciÃ³n", owner, 2026-11-13): while iterating, run only your part of the smoke, with no window, and build only the Editor target. The full smoke and the game-target build are run once, at the end, by the architect. (Added to this brief after it was first handed over; the implementer was told by message.)
- `powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File Tools/Jam/Build-Jam.ps1`: the Editor target; must end in `Result: Succeeded`. Do not build `-Target Glyphbound`.
- `powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File Tools/Jam/Test-Jam.ps1` with the anchored filter of the weapons tests (read the script's header for its parameters; filters look like `^Glyphbound.Weapons.`): all green.
- `powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File Tools/Jam/Smoke-Jam.ps1 -Only shotgun -Offscreen`: must end in `done=True crashed=False LogGB errors=0`. `-Only` keeps the steps whose name starts with that prefix, plus the quit step: name every step of your block `shotgun: ...`, and make the block work on its own (its first step gives the player the shotgun and equips it; it sets up its own enchant, targets and weapon switches). Do not run the full `Smoke-Jam.ps1`.
- Open your four screenshots in `Glyphbound/Saved/Screenshots/Smoke/` (the PNG files; enlarge the gun's area if you need to) and tune the framing and the poses until: the gun reads like the rifle's framing, the hologram and the energy bar are visible in `15a`, the pump is clearly back in `15b`, and `15d` shows the underside with the port and a shell on its way in. The first screenshot after a material change can come out tan and grainy (shaders compiling): run the filtered smoke again before judging it.
- You will not have run the full smoke, so you cannot confirm that the pistol's and the rifle's lines are unchanged: say so in your report and list every code path of theirs that you touched.
- Do not package.

## Final report
The usual five points of `implementer_rules.md`, plus: the art values you ended with for the shotgun; what each of the four screenshots shows; every reader of `SpareMagazines` you found and what you did about each; the test names; and anything you could not judge from still images (the pump's and the reload's timing and feel need a person playing).

  Design\GDD.md:42:
  Design\GDD.md:43:## 0. Baseline
> Design\GDD.md:44:At its core this is a **classic first-person RPG plus a classic first-person shooter**. Anything 
not stated here behaves as players expect from those two genres (magazines and reload, ammo pickups, standard shooter 
gamepad layout, etc.). This document only lists what is specific to our game.
  Design\GDD.md:45:
  Design\GDD.md:46:## 0.1 Technical direction
  Design\GDD.md:47:- **Engine:** Unreal Engine 5.9.0, pinned until submission. **Single player only.**
  Design\GDD.md:48:- **Architect autonomy:** nobody on the team is a programmer. **Every technical and engineering 
decision is the architect's**, taken without asking the owner: code architecture, C++ vs. Blueprints, plugins, tools, 
data formats, optimization. Decide, build, and log the decision and its reason in the dev log. When a design detail is 
missing, pick a reasonable provisional value, mark it provisional here, and keep going; ask the owner only when the 
game's vision would change.
  Design\GDD.md:49:- **Time first:** iteration speed matters more than elegance. Unused plugins off, Live Coding for 
`.cpp` changes, only the touched tests on each change, the full suite (under 30 s) once before the daily package. 
Rules in `CLAUDE.md` ("Tiempo de iteración").
  Design\GDD.md:50:- **Code (decided by the architect, 2026-11-12):** C++ only, one module, no Blueprint graphs; 
structure in `Design/ARCHITECTURE.md`. **Unreal's Gameplay Ability System is not used**: a small stat-and-modifier 
system, one damage function and one status-effect component cover the same needs with far less setup.
> Design\GDD.md:51:- **Tuning data (decided by the architect):** every gameplay number lives in text files, 
`Glyphbound/Content/Data/*.json` (spells, weapons, enemies, items, skills, player, QTE...). They ship as loose files: 
the owner can change numbers in a packaged build with a text editor, and `gb.ReloadData` in the console reloads them 
while playing. This replaces the data tables suggested earlier (git only carries text in this team, and AI agents edit 
text reliably).
  Design\GDD.md:52:- **Test level (decided by the architect):** the day-1 test arena is built in code on the engine's 
empty map, so no binary map asset is needed yet. Real levels come later from the level designers.
  Design\GDD.md:53:- **Placeholders first:** pixel art and 3D models are made by other team members on machines on the 
local network. **The architect never waits for art:** it uses quickly generated placeholders and swaps them later.
  Design\GDD.md:54:- **Placeholder numbers:** the architect puts provisional values (damage, mana, cooldowns, QTE 
length and time limit…) in data tables; the owner tunes them later.
  Design\GDD.md:55:- **PIE is human-only:** Play In Editor is always run by a person. AI agents do not run PIE; they 
compile, package and run tests, then ask a human to play-test. (Also in `CLAUDE.md`.)
  Design\GDD.md:56:- **Era travel:** a fade with an effect when crossing a portal; the transition hides loading the 
other era's version of the map.
  Design\GDD.md:57:- **Saving:** checkpoints only. No save to disk between sessions.
  Design\GDD.md:58:- **Size:** the jam page says "keep it under 1 GB" for desktop zips, but **bigger builds are 
allowed** if uploaded with butler (installed on CENTRAL) or Blendo Itch Uploader. The Official Rules PDF sets no size 
limit. So 1 GB is a target, not a hard limit.
  Design\GDD.md:59:- **NVIDIA RTX Award:** we intend to compete. It is the "best Render Royalty entry built in Unreal 
Engine" that uses at least one of: an NvRTX feature (RTX Mega Geometry, RTX Direct Illumination, ReSTIR GI, ReSTIR PT 
path tracing, LSS Hair), **DLSS 4.5 (Unreal Engine)**, or NVIDIA ACE; plus the NVIDIA questions at submission and 
listing the tool in "Tools you used".
  Design\GDD.md:60:- **Modifier rule (Official Rules §10):** a team can win **only one modifier** (Glass Box, Render 
Royalty, Best Team…) unless no other eligible winner remains. We still enter all that apply.
  Design\GDD.md:61:
  Design\GDD.md:62:## 1. One-line pitch
  Design\GDD.md:63:A fast first-person **shooter/RPG hybrid**: the player, a medieval mage, jumps through fixed 
portals between a **pixel-art medieval past** full of magic and a **3D cyberpunk future** full of guns, on the same 
map in two times, and wins by **mixing spells and firearms** rather than sticking to one style.
  Design\GDD.md:134:    | Flamethrower | Intense Heat | +12% damage |
  Design\GDD.md:135:    | Lightning rune | Expanded Sigil | +10% blast radius |
  Design\GDD.md:136:    | Static orb | Persistent Charge | +15% orb duration |
  Design\GDD.md:137:    | Ice shield | Enduring Guard | +15% maximum shield duration (Glacier Wall stays the 
"toughness" perk) |
  Design\GDD.md:138:    | Ice floor | Deep Freeze | +5 percentage points of slow, with a cap so enemies are never 
fully stopped |
> Design\GDD.md:139:  - **Weapon tree:** damage, fire rate, **recoil** (better aim), **damage by distance** (shotgun 
stronger up close, rifle stronger far away), **headshot damage**, reload speed, healing on precise kills, enchant 
build-up. Layout in §18 "Weapon tree".
  Design\GDD.md:140:- **One shared pool of skill points** for both trees.
  Design\GDD.md:141:- **Perks can be reset** (owner, 2026-11-12, to test them): a "Reset skills" option on the skill 
tree screen removes every unlocked node and gives back all the points spent on them. Provisional details in section 18.
  Design\GDD.md:142:- **Skill points** are given at specific points, mainly for defeating enemies:
  Design\GDD.md:143:  - defeating each **sub-boss**;
  Design\GDD.md:144:  - defeating each **boss**;
  Design\GDD.md:145:  - **clearing a wave** of enemies.
  Design\GDD.md:146:
  Design\GDD.md:147:## 6. Magic (4 elements + weapon enchant)
  Design\GDD.md:148:Each element has 3 entries. Air is passives only; the other three are active spells.
  Design\GDD.md:149:
  Design\GDD.md:150:| Element | 1 | 2 | 3 |
  Design\GDD.md:151:|---|---|---|---|
  Design\GDD.md:240:
  Design\GDD.md:241:### Held slots
  Design\GDD.md:242:- The player carries **one gun and one staff.** Never two guns or two staffs.
  Design\GDD.md:243:- Once obtained, the **elemental sword is a special third slot.**
  Design\GDD.md:244:- Switch with the **mouse wheel or Alt**; on gamepad, with a **D-pad arrow** (which one: **TBD**).
> Design\GDD.md:245:- Staff in hand: basic magic attack + the 5 spells. Gun in hand: shoot + aim + reload + the 
enchant.
  Design\GDD.md:246:
  Design\GDD.md:247:### Staffs (medieval, pixel art)
  Design\GDD.md:248:- With a staff in hand, **primary fire = basic magic attack**. The attack **depends on which 
staff** the player holds, so there is more than one staff.
  Design\GDD.md:249:- Staffs are found in **chests** around the map.
  Design\GDD.md:250:- **A staff that is picked up goes to the inventory** (owner, 2026-11-12). Nothing is dropped on 
the floor and the staff in hand is not replaced: the player opens the inventory and equips the new one by hand. 
(Provisional, architect: guns and the sword follow the same rule; the first weapon of each kind goes straight to its 
empty slot.)
  Design\GDD.md:251:- Staff list and what each basic attack does: **TBD**.
  Design\GDD.md:252:
  Design\GDD.md:253:### Guns (cyberpunk, 3D)
  Design\GDD.md:254:- Pistol, shotgun, assault rifle.
  Design\GDD.md:255:- The **first gun** is dropped by one of the first armed orcs in the future (§4). The **shotgun 
and the rifle** are dropped later by enemies, picked up from the floor.
  Design\GDD.md:256:- With a gun in hand: **primary fire = shoot**, **secondary = aim**.
  Design\GDD.md:257:- **Moving costs accuracy (owner, 2026-11-12):** shots spread more while the player moves than 
while standing still; the crosshair opens to show it. See §18.
> Design\GDD.md:258:- **Magazines and reload**, like any shooter.
> Design\GDD.md:259:- **Gun models (v0.25):** the pistol is the first real 3D model (made with Tripo on ASSETS, 
finished in Blender on CENTRAL); the shotgun and rifle keep placeholder shapes until their models exist. The pistol's 
trigger moves on each shot and its magazine slides out and back in during the reload. **Glow (provisional, 
architect):** the gun's glowing parts show its own green when not enchanted; while the enchant runs they take the 
element's colour, and the energy bar on the side shows the enchant time left (full when cast, empty when it ends). 
**Owner, v0.30:** the bar's 13 cells show the time left rounded up, so the last cell goes dark exactly when the 
enchant ends (before, it went dark half a cell early); the weak-point light is half as long as before: full for 0.06 
s, then a 0.15 s fade (`PistolWeakPointFlashHold` / `PistolWeakPointFlashFade` in `Game.json`).
  Design\GDD.md:260:- **Ammo works like classic tactical shooters:** each gun has a fixed number of spare magazines, 
and each magazine a fixed number of bullets.
  Design\GDD.md:261:- **All ammo refills completely every time the player travels between eras.**
  Design\GDD.md:262:- Between refills, ammo is **picked up from the floor** where enemies drop it.
  Design\GDD.md:263:- Level design keeps ammo tight: never infinite, never starving.
  Design\GDD.md:264:- **Electric grenade** (throwable).
  Design\GDD.md:265:
  Design\GDD.md:266:### Armor
  Design\GDD.md:267:- Found in **chests**, rarely.
  Design\GDD.md:268:- **One armor slot = one full armor.** No separate helmet, arms or chest pieces.
  Design\GDD.md:269:- Known effect: some armor changes the QTE time-slow amount (§7). Other stats: **TBD**.
  Design\GDD.md:270:
  Design\GDD.md:271:### Chests
  Design\GDD.md:294:| Look | Mouse | Right stick |
  Design\GDD.md:295:| Primary attack (staff: basic magic attack; gun: shoot) | Left click | R2 / RT |
  Design\GDD.md:296:| Aim (gun in hand) | Right click | L2 / LT |
  Design\GDD.md:297:| Spell slot 1 (staff in hand) | Right click | L1 / LB |
  Design\GDD.md:298:| Spell slots 2–5 (staff in hand) | 1, 2, 3, 4 | Hold L2 / LT + Cross, Circle, Square, Triangle 
(A, B, X, Y) |
> Design\GDD.md:299:| Reload (gun) | R | Standard shooter layout |
> Design\GDD.md:300:| Weapon enchant (gun in hand only) | T (R is reload) | L1 / LB (free with a gun in hand) |
  Design\GDD.md:301:| Switch staff ↔ gun (↔ sword) | Mouse wheel or Alt | D-pad up |
  Design\GDD.md:302:| Jump | Space | Cross (A) |
  Design\GDD.md:303:| Dash | Ctrl | Circle (B) |
  Design\GDD.md:304:| Quick-use item (grenade / potion / injection share one slot) | F | R1 / RB |
  Design\GDD.md:305:| Cycle the quick-use item | Shift | D-pad left / right |
  Design\GDD.md:306:| Interact (chests, portals, objects) | G | Triangle (Y) |
  Design\GDD.md:307:| Block: raise the summoned ice shield (staff or sword in hand; provisional keys) | Q (hold) | L2 
/ LT (hold) |
  Design\GDD.md:308:| QTE part 1 | W A S D | Face buttons |
  Design\GDD.md:309:| QTE part 2 | Mouse gesture | Right stick |
  Design\GDD.md:310:| Inventory | Tab | Options / View |
  Design\GDD.md:311:| Skill trees | K (also a tab inside the inventory) | From the inventory |
  Design\GDD.md:312:| Pause | Esc | Start / Menu |
  Design\GDD.md:450:### Rules
  Design\GDD.md:451:- **Jumps commit to their direction (owner, 2026-11-12):** like a real jump, the direction cannot 
be changed in the air (air control 0; the same when walking off a ledge). The direction is chosen at takeoff: the 
movement keys held at that moment. The second jump can choose a new direction and then commits again. Provisional 
details (architect): a jump from standing with a direction held leaves at 60% of walking speed at least; the second 
jump with a direction held leaves at walking speed at least, and with no direction held it keeps the current drift. 
The dash still works in the air and is not affected.
  Design\GDD.md:452:- **Double jump on day 1:** the player starts knowing the air passive "Double jump" so it can be 
tested now. In the real game it is learned in the air zone (§6); remove it from `Player.json` `StartingSpells` then.
  Design\GDD.md:453:- **Dash:** available from the start (the air passive only improves it). It goes in the movement 
direction (forward when standing still), works in the air, gives no invulnerability, and does not refill jumps.
  Design\GDD.md:454:- **Owning weapons:** decided by the owner for staffs (section 8): picked-up weapons go to the 
inventory and are equipped by hand. Provisional part: guns and the sword follow the same rule; each owned gun keeps 
its own ammo while stored.
> Design\GDD.md:455:- **Reload:** the bullets left in the old magazine are lost (classic tactical style). A gun 
reloads by itself when its magazine runs dry. Ammo pickups give one spare magazine, up to the gun's maximum.
> Design\GDD.md:456:  - **The shotgun loads shell by shell (owner, 2026-11-12).** One shell goes in at a time; nothing 
is lost by reloading early, and the reload can stop part-way. Provisional (architect): its reserve is counted in 
shells (4 magazines x 6 = 24, the same total as before); each shell takes `ReloadTime / MagazineSize` (0.33 s), so a 
full reload from empty still takes the 2 s the balance tables use and Rapid Reload speeds it the same way; pressing 
fire during the reload stops it and shoots as soon as a shell is in (the shell on its way in is not lost: it stays in 
the reserve); an ammo pickup gives one magazine's worth of shells (6); an empty shotgun still starts reloading by 
itself.
  Design\GDD.md:457:  - **The shotgun's forend pumps after every shot (owner, 2026-11-12).** Provisional (architect): 
it is only the look; the time between shots stays `FireInterval` (0.8 s) and the pump plays inside it.
  Design\GDD.md:458:- **Sustained spells (flamethrower), changed by the owner (2026-11-12):** after the QTE the 
flamethrower runs **while its spell key is held**, until the key is released or the mana runs out (also on weapon 
switch or death). No time limit. Provisional (architect): it pays a smaller cost at the start (10 mana, always spent, 
like every QTE spell) and then drains 15 mana per second; if the key was already released when the QTE ends, it still 
burns for a short minimum (0.75 s) so the cast is never wasted; the cooldown starts at the cast. All values in 
`Spells.json` (`ManaCost`, `ManaPerSecond`, `MinDuration`).
  Design\GDD.md:459:- **Strong hit interrupts the flamethrower (owner, 2026-11-12):** a single hit that takes **30% or 
more of the player's maximum health** stops it at once, and the camera shakes. The in-game description says "a strong 
hit", never the number. Provisional (architect): measured against maximum health (not current health), on the damage 
actually taken after the ice shield and armor; the threshold is a spell parameter (`InterruptHitFraction`, so any 
sustained spell can use it); the shake is short (about 0.35 s, a few degrees, values in `Game.json`), only moves the 
view, never the aim; the mana already spent is not refunded and the cooldown is unchanged.
  Design\GDD.md:460:- **Spread while moving (owner, 2026-11-12; numbers by the architect, provisional):** each gun 
adds `MoveSpread` (hip fire) or `AimMoveSpread` (aiming) degrees to its spread, scaled by the player's horizontal 
speed over the current walk speed (0 standing still, full bonus at full walk speed or faster; aiming already lowers 
the walk speed, so a slow aimed walk counts as full). Pistol 1.2 -> 3.7 hip, 0.25 -> 1.25 aimed; shotgun 6 -> 9, 4.5 
-> 6.5; rifle 1.8 -> 4.8, 0.35 -> 1.55 (`Weapons.json`, modifiable by perks as `Weapon.<Id>.MoveSpread`). Jumping 
counts by its horizontal speed only.
  Design\GDD.md:461:- **Spell tree (owner, v0.27):** four columns, top to bottom. **Fire:** Searing Fireball -> Wide 
Blast, Lingering Flames, Intense Heat. **Lightning:** Overcharge, Expanded Sigil, Persistent Charge. **Ice:** Glacier 
Wall, Enduring Guard, Deep Freeze. **Casting:** Frugal Casting -> Quick Recall. Only those two links stay; every other 
spell perk has no prerequisite (Frugal Casting no longer needs Overcharge). Costs unchanged (Frugal Casting and Quick 
Recall 2 points, the rest 1).
> Design\GDD.md:462:- **Weapon tree (owner, v0.24):** Heavy Rounds (+10% gun damage) on top. **Handling** branch 
(left): Quick Trigger (-8% time between shots) -> Rapid Reload (-10% reload time per rank, -30% at 3) and Steady Grip 
(-20% recoil per rank, now pitch and yaw). **Precision** branch (right): Marksman (+0.25x head / weak point damage) -> 
Precision Recovery and Arcane Conduit (+10% elemental build-up from enchanted weapons per rank, +30% at 3; the sword's 
own element does not count). **Specializations** (bottom row, all needing only Heavy Rounds, so no gun is favored): 
Deadeye (pistol, +0.25x head / weak point damage per rank: 2.0 -> 2.25 / 2.5 / 2.75), Point Blank (shotgun, +0.2x up 
close), Long Shot (rifle, +0.2x far; owner, v0.29, was +0.15x). Every perk: 3 ranks, 1 point per rank (Marksman 2, 
Precision Recovery 2 since v0.27). **Precision Recovery:** killing an enemy with a head / weak point hit heals a fixed 
**3% of max health**, with an internal cooldown of **6 / 5 / 4 s** by rank; once per enemy death (a shotgun's pellets 
never heal more than once), never for a head hit that does not kill (in a horde game that would be near-unlimited 
healing). Provisional (architect): the prerequisites inside each branch (the owner's sketch only shows the arrows 
Heavy Rounds -> both branches, Quick Trigger -> Rapid Reload, Marksman -> Precision Recovery; Steady Grip hangs from 
Quick Trigger and Arcane Conduit from Marksman); only the killing blow counts, from any weapon, spells excluded (they 
never flag weak points).
  Design\GDD.md:463:- **Flamethrower damage ticks (owner, 2026-11-12):** damage is dealt in fixed time steps, never 
per frame, so it does not depend on the frame rate: one tick every **0.3 s** (`TickInterval`), each dealing 
`DamagePerSecond x 0.3` (the mana drain follows the same ticks). If a frame is longer than a tick, the missed ticks 
still happen.
  Design\GDD.md:464:- **New spell perks (owner's list, placement by the architect):** in the spell tree, each under 
its element's column (fire, lightning, ice), with no prerequisite so they can be tested right away. Deep Freeze: the 
ice floor slows by 50% at normal QTE power (the slow effect, `StatusEffects.json`), so +5 points per rank makes 55 / 
60 / 65%; the cap is **80%** (also reached by a perfect QTE with 3 ranks). The cap is a general rule: any spell 
parameter can declare a `Cap` in `Spells.json`, applied after QTE scaling and perks.
  Design\GDD.md:465:- **Ice shield QTE:** part 1 (power) scales its duration and health; part 2 (reach) scales how 
much frontal damage it blocks.
  Design\GDD.md:466:- **Modifier keys by weapon type:** besides one weapon (`Weapon.Pistol.Damage`) and all weapons 
(`Weapon.All.Damage`), a modifier can target a slot: `Weapon.Gun.*`, `Weapon.Staff.*`, `Weapon.Sword.*`. The enchant 
uses `Weapon.Gun.Damage`, so it never buffs the staff or the sword.
  Design\GDD.md:467:- **Explosions and enemy melee need a clear line** to their target: no damage through walls or 
through the ice wall.
  Design\GDD.md:468:- **Enemies and cover (after the first play-test):** enemies walk around obstacles. A melee enemy 
only starts its attack when it sees the player; a ranged enemy that loses sight stops aiming and moves until it sees 
the player again. Enemies do not walk off ledges on their own; one that falls out of the map dies without drops.
  Design\GDD.md:469:- **Projectile explosions push enemies back** (knockback is a spell parameter, scaled by QTE 
power); they do not push the player.
  Design\GDD.md:470:- **Falling:** the kill height is 2,500 cm below the floor of the level (`Game.json` `KillZ`).
  Design\GDD.md:471:- **Weapon enchant QTE:** part 1 scales how long the buff lasts; part 2 scales how strong it is. 
Only one enchant at a time. The enchanted gun deals the element's damage type and builds up that element's bar on 
every hit (§6 "Elemental build-up"; changed by the owner on 2026-11-12, it used to apply a small effect on each hit).
  Design\GDD.md:472:- **Spell walls:** the fire wall does not block; it damages enemies that touch it and builds up 
fire. The ice wall blocks movement and shots and has health; anyone's shots can break it, the player's included. 
Enemies do not attack walls on purpose yet.
  Design\GDD.md:473:- **QTE:** a wrong key consumes its step. A gesture is judged when the hand stops for a moment or 
the stroke is clearly complete. If both parts are fully missed the spell fizzles, and the mana is still spent and the 
cooldown still starts.
  Design\GDD.md:474:- **New spells** go to the first free loadout slot.
  Design\GDD.md:500:- Every spell number in `Spells.json`; the v0.28 values and their reasons are in §19.
  Design\GDD.md:501:
  Design\GDD.md:502:## 19. Balance (v0.28, architect, 2026-11-12)
  Design\GDD.md:503:The owner's brief: fights a little long but not too long; a fast shooter where enemies die all the 
time in the waves; no bullet sponges, except an enemy that is slow and hits softly; two Valkyries and then a final 
boss fought with every tool; no levels; not enough skill points for the whole tree, but enough for a well-armed mage 
plus one gun and a half well upgraded. Approved by the owner: waves of 60-90 s, Valkyries of about 2 min, the Entity 
about 3 min, 36 skill points.
  Design\GDD.md:504:
> Design\GDD.md:505:**Every number here is provisional and lives in `Glyphbound/Content/Data/*.json`.** Nothing needs 
a recompile: edit the file and run `gb.ReloadData` in the console (it also works in the packaged game). `node 
Tools/Balance/balance_model.js` prints every table below from the data, so after changing a number the tables can be 
checked again in a second. The numbers were reviewed by a second model (GPT-6-Astra, medium effort) before the first 
build; what it found is at the end.
  Design\GDD.md:506:
  Design\GDD.md:507:### Method
  Design\GDD.md:508:- **Three anchors.** The player has 100 health and 100 mana for the whole game. The reference 
damage is the base pistol on the body: 80 per second. An enemy's health is "seconds it should last x the damage a 
player really deals".
  Design\GDD.md:509:- **Real damage is about half the paper damage:** the model takes 70% of the time attacking, 
60-75% of the shots hitting and a quarter of the hits on the head. Each base gun then deals about 40 per second.
  Design\GDD.md:510:- **Builds are calculated, not guessed.** The calculator applies the perks of six reference builds 
(one per point of the game, mixing both trees) to the weapon and spell data: about 65 real damage per second from the 
gun at the start, 86 at 16 points, 99-107 at the Valkyries, 117 at the Entity. A perk's percentage stacks in a 
straight line with its rank (+10% per rank is +30% at rank 3).
  Design\GDD.md:511:- **Ammo runs out.** A fight uses the gun until the ammo carried and dropped is gone, then the 
staff or the sword. Spells run all the time, as often as the mana allows: mana only comes back after 1.5 s without 
spending, so the steady rhythm is one 15-mana spell every 4 seconds.
  Design\GDD.md:512:- **The QTE costs real time.** Each cast is about 2 real seconds while the world runs at 10%, so a 
fight with many spells lasts longer on the clock than in game time. Lengths below are real seconds.
  Design\GDD.md:513:- **The QTE maps its score in a straight line from Min (score 0) to Max (perfect).** `Base` is the 
value with the QTE off. For damage, Min is half of Base and Max one and a half. The model uses a score of 0.75.
  Design\GDD.md:514:
  Design\GDD.md:515:### Player
  Design\GDD.md:516:| | Value | Why |
  Design\GDD.md:517:|---|---|---|
  Design\GDD.md:518:| Health | 100, +3/s after 6 s without damage | No regeneration inside a fight; a clean disengage 
(dash, ice wall) is rewarded |
  Design\GDD.md:519:| Mana | 100, +6/s after 1.5 s without spending | Six or seven spells in a burst, then one every 4 
s |
  Design\GDD.md:520:| Largest single hit | 32 (sub-bosses) | Never more than 45% of the bar: no one-shots. 30% or more 
is a "strong hit" and stops the flamethrower (the old brute hit 28 and never did) |
  Design\GDD.md:521:
  Design\GDD.md:522:### Weapons (no perks)
> Design\GDD.md:523:| Weapon | Hit | Sustained damage/s (with reloads) | Role |
  Design\GDD.md:524:|---|---|---|---|
  Design\GDD.md:525:| Apprentice staff | 12 every 0.35 s | 34, free | The fallback that never runs out: about half a 
gun |
  Design\GDD.md:526:| Ember staff | 22 every 0.6 s | 37 + Ignite, free | Slower, sets enemies on fire |
  Design\GDD.md:527:| Pistol | 20 every 0.25 s, 15 rounds, 6 spare magazines | 61 (121 on the head) | Precision: the 
best head bonus (x2) |
  Design\GDD.md:528:| Shotgun | 8 x 10 every 0.8 s, 6 shells, 4 spare | 71 up close, 14 far | Risk: one shell kills a 
soldier at arm's length |
  Design\GDD.md:529:| Rifle | 12 every 0.1 s, 30 rounds, 5 spare | 60 near, 86 far | Range and volume; less accurate, 
weak up close |
  Design\GDD.md:530:| Elemental sword | 50 every 0.5 s | 100, free | Late game, melee risk, hits a 90 degree arc |
  Design\GDD.md:531:
  Design\GDD.md:532:The three guns come out even (about 40 real damage per second each). The rifle was the outlier 
before (140 burst against 82 and 94).
  Design\GDD.md:533:
  Design\GDD.md:534:**Ammo.** A full load really deals 2,600 to 4,300 damage depending on the build: about one long 
wave shooting only. Magazine drops are set so that a base gun gets back about half of the rounds it spends in the 
future and a quarter in the past, and a gun with 16 points in it gets back almost all of them in the future and half 
in the past. Drop chance of a magazine in the future: swarm 6%, soldier 12%, elite 30%, tank 70%, sub-boss 3 
magazines; in the past: 3%, 6%, 15%, 35%, sub-boss 2. The past pushes the player to the staff and the spells; nobody 
is ever stuck, because the staff is free.
  Design\GDD.md:535:
  Design\GDD.md:585:| Entity | 2 phases | 16,000 | about 190 (it has the sword) |
  Design\GDD.md:586:
  Design\GDD.md:587:`Encounters.json` holds one ready-made encounter of each kind (placeholders for the level 
designers: `Opening_*`, `Wave_Past_1..3`, `Wave_Future_1..3`, `SubBoss_*`, `Zone_Fire/Lightning/Ice/Air`, 
`Valkyrie_*`, `Entity`), besides the two shorter test waves of the arena.
  Design\GDD.md:588:
  Design\GDD.md:589:### Skill points
> Design\GDD.md:590:Both trees cost 78 points (weapons 36, spells 42). **Budget: 36 points before the Entity (46%).** 
Example: Heavy Rounds, Quick Trigger, Rapid Reload, Marksman and Deadeye at rank 3 (18 points, a finished pistol), 
Point Blank 3 (the shotgun half-way), Frugal Casting 3 (6 points) and three spell upgrades at rank 3 (9 points): a 
build, not the whole tree.
  Design\GDD.md:591:
  Design\GDD.md:592:| Source | Points |
  Design\GDD.md:593:|---|---|
  Design\GDD.md:594:| Start | 2 |
  Design\GDD.md:595:| Each of the two opening fights | 1 |
  Design\GDD.md:596:| Each wave (6) | 1 |
  Design\GDD.md:597:| Each sub-boss (2) | 2 |
  Design\GDD.md:598:| Each elemental zone (4) | 3 |
  Design\GDD.md:599:| Each Valkyrie | 5 |
  Design\GDD.md:600:| **Total** | **36** |
  Design\GDD.md:601:
  Design\GDD.md:602:If the number of encounters changes, the points per encounter are adjusted to keep the total (the 
calculator prints the sum and flags it).
  Design\GDD.md:606:- **Long Shot: applied by the owner (v0.29).** It was +0.15x per rank (+39% at long range against 
+60% for Point Blank); at +0.2x per rank the rifle gets +52%.
  Design\GDD.md:607:- **Free skill reset:** answered by the owner, it will not be in the final version, so the 
36-point budget is a real build choice.
  Design\GDD.md:608:- **Chest contents** of the test arena are still written in code (`GBTestArena.cpp`); they move to 
data when real levels exist.
  Design\GDD.md:609:
  Design\GDD.md:610:### Values moved from code to data in v0.28
> Design\GDD.md:611:Enemy AI fractions, projectile lifetime and knockback lift, grenade blast falloff and bounce, the 
fire wall's touch margin, the ice shield's block arc, player acceleration, friction and look speed, what a respawn and 
an era travel restore, and what a failed QTE costs. All keep their old value; the list is in the day entry. A spell 
already in the world keeps the numbers it was cast with: cast it again after `gb.ReloadData`.
  Design\GDD.md:612:
  Design\GDD.md:613:### Second opinion (GPT-6-Astra, before the first build)
  Design\GDD.md:614:Taken: builds calculated from the perks instead of a flat multiplier (the old guess was half of 
the real damage); the ammo limit inside the boss maths (the Valkyries went from 9,000-10,000 to 7,200-7,600); the real 
time of the QTE; mana that only regenerates between casts; enemy attack cycles with their windup; lower magazine drops 
(a mid-game pistol was getting back more rounds than it spent); longer cooldowns for the ice wall, shield and floor; 
the lingering ice floor slow; the orb's range; a fifth sub-wave; and two code notes (the staff ignored the new 
projectile lifetime key). Not taken: an ice shield cooldown of 34 s (too harsh for a player without perks; its health 
already limits it) and a magazine drop of 8% for the armored orc (a base gun would starve; 12% keeps a specialized 
pistol just under break-even).

+	// Shotgun block of the smoke run (round-by-round reload and pump).
+	static AGBGun* ShotgunInHand(AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
+	{
+		AGBGun* Gun = Cast<AGBGun>(Player->GetEquipment()->GetActiveItem());
+		return (Gun && Gun->GetWeaponId() == FName(TEXT("Shotgun"))) ? Gun : nullptr;
+	}
+	static TWeakObjectPtr<AGBEnemyCharacter> ShotgunFlashTarget;
+	static FGBAmmoState ShotgunAmmoBefore;
+	// The reload from 3 shells: the game time it started and the time one shell takes (ReloadTime moves with the Rapid Reload perk).
+	static double ShotgunReloadStartSim = 0.0;
+	static float ShotgunCycleSeconds = 0.333f;
+	// Everything at rest: no reload, the shell hidden inside the port, the pump home, the root at its rest pose.
+	static void CheckShotgunAtRest(const TCHAR* When, AGBGun* Gun)
+	{
+		const bool bOk = !Gun->IsReloading() && !Gun->IsViewModelMagazineVisible() && Gun->GetViewModelMagazineOffsetCm() < 0.05f
+			&& Gun->GetViewModelPumpOffsetCm() < 0.05f && Gun->GetViewModelRootOffsetCm() < 0.05f && Gun->GetViewModelRootAngleDeg() < 0.5f;
+		UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun at rest (%s): reloading=%d shell visible=%d shell %.2f cm pump %.2f cm root %.2f cm %.2f deg"), When,
+			Gun->IsReloading() ? 1 : 0, Gun->IsViewModelMagazineVisible() ? 1 : 0, Gun->GetViewModelMagazineOffsetCm(),
+			Gun->GetViewModelPumpOffsetCm(), Gun->GetViewModelRootOffsetCm(), Gun->GetViewModelRootAngleDeg());
+		if (!bOk)
+		{
+			UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun rest FAILED (%s): the shell, the pump or the gun is not at rest"), When);
+		}
+	}
+
 	static void Shot(const TCHAR* Name)
 	{
 		const FString Path = FPaths::ProjectSavedDir() / TEXT("Screenshots/Smoke") / (FString(Name) + TEXT(".png"));

+		// ---- Shotgun: model, pump, shell-by-shell reload (feedback15). Its clock runs on from the rifle block and is wound back at the end. ----
+		const float SG = 61.7f + S;
+		Add(SG, TEXT("shotgun: own it, equip it in the gun slot, full ammo, no enchant"), [](UWorld*, AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
+		{
+			Player->GetEquipment()->GiveWeapon(TEXT("Shotgun"), false, false);
+			Player->GetEquipment()->EquipOwnedWeapon(TEXT("Shotgun"));
+			Player->GetEquipment()->SwitchTo(EGBHeldSlot::Gun);
+			if (UGBStatusEffectComponent* Effects = Player->GetStatusEffects())
+			{
+				Effects->RemoveAllEffects();
+			}
+			if (AGBGun* Gun = Cast<AGBGun>(Player->GetEquipment()->GetActiveItem()))
+			{
+				Gun->RefillAmmo();
+			}
+		});
+		Add(SG + 0.4f, TEXT("shotgun: meshes and materials in hand, display 6, hip screenshot"), [](UWorld*, AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
+		{
+			AGBGun* Gun = ShotgunInHand(Player);
+			if (!Gun)
+			{
+				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun: no shotgun in hand"));
+				return;
+			}
+			const int32 Meshes = Gun->GetViewModelMeshCount();
+			const int32 Mids = Gun->GetViewModelMIDCount();
+			const FGBAmmoState Ammo = Gun->GetAmmo();
+			UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun viewmodel meshes=%d mids=%d pump=%d display=%s ammo %d / spare rounds %d"), Meshes, Mids,
+				Gun->HasPump() ? 1 : 0, *Gun->GetViewModelDisplayText(), Ammo.InMagazine, Ammo.SpareMagazines);
+			if (Meshes != 6 || Mids <= 6 || !Gun->HasPump())
+			{
+				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun viewmodel FAILED: meshes=%d (expected 6) mids=%d (expected more than 6) pump=%d"), Meshes, Mids, Gun->HasPump() ? 1 : 0);
+			}
+			if (Gun->GetViewModelDisplayText() != TEXT("6") || Ammo.InMagazine != 6 || Ammo.SpareMagazines != 24 || !Gun->ReloadsPerRound())
+			{
+				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun ammo FAILED: display '%s', %d in the gun, %d spare (expected 6, 6, 24 rounds) perRound=%d"),
+					*Gun->GetViewModelDisplayText(), Ammo.InMagazine, Ammo.SpareMagazines, Gun->ReloadsPerRound() ? 1 : 0);
+			}
+			CheckShotgunAtRest(TEXT("hip"), Gun);
+			Shot(TEXT("15a_shotgun_hip"));
+		});
+		Add(SG + 0.9f, TEXT("shotgun shot: kick, muzzle flash, one round less"), [](UWorld*, AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
+		{
+			AGBGun* Gun = ShotgunInHand(Player);
+			if (!Gun)
+			{
+				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun shot: no shotgun in hand"));
+				return;
+			}
+			Gun->StartPrimary();
+			Gun->StopPrimary();
+			const bool bVisible = Gun->IsMuzzleFlashVisible();
+			const float Kick = Gun->GetViewModelKickBackCm();
+			UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun shot: muzzle flash visible=%d kick=%.2f ammo=%d"), bVisible ? 1 : 0, Kick, Gun->GetAmmo().InMagazine);
+			if (!bVisible || Kick <= 0.1f || Gun->GetAmmo().InMagazine != 5)
+			{
+				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun shot FAILED: visible=%d kick=%.2f ammo=%d right after a shot (expected a flash, a kick and 5 rounds)"),
+					bVisible ? 1 : 0, Kick, Gun->GetAmmo().InMagazine);
+			}
+		});
+		Add(SG + 1.27f, TEXT("shotgun pump: slid back during the interval, display 5"), [](UWorld*, AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
+		{
+			AGBGun* Gun = ShotgunInHand(Player);
+			if (!Gun)
+			{
+				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun pump: no shotgun in hand"));
+				return;
+			}
+			const float Pump = Gun->GetViewModelPumpOffsetCm();
+			const FString Text = Gun->GetViewModelDisplayText();
+			UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun pump 0.37 s after the shot: %.2f cm from rest, display=%s"), Pump, *Text);
+			if (Pump < 1.2f)
+			{
+				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun pump FAILED: only %.2f cm from rest in the middle of the interval (expected it back, about 4 cm)"), Pump);
+			}
+			if (Text != TEXT("5"))
+			{
+				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun display FAILED: '%s' after one shot (expected 5)"), *Text);
+			}
+		});
+		Add(SG + 1.85f, TEXT("shotgun pump: home again before the next shot is allowed"), [](UWorld*, AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
+		{
+			AGBGun* Gun = ShotgunInHand(Player);
+			if (!Gun)
+			{
+				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun pump: no shotgun in hand"));
+				return;
+			}
+			CheckShotgunAtRest(TEXT("after the pump"), Gun);
+		});
+		Add(SG + 2.0f, TEXT("shotgun pump: pinned at the back of its travel, screenshot"), [](UWorld*, AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
+		{
+			AGBGun* Gun = ShotgunInHand(Player);
+			if (!Gun)
+			{
+				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun pump: no shotgun in hand"));
+				return;
+			}
+			Gun->SetPumpPreview(1.f);
+			const float Pump = Gun->GetViewModelPumpOffsetCm();
+			const float RootCm = Gun->GetViewModelRootOffsetCm();
+			UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun pump pinned: %.2f cm from rest, root %.2f cm %.2f deg, hand shift %s"), Pump, RootCm,
+				Gun->GetViewModelRootAngleDeg(), *Gun->GetPumpHandShift().ToString());
+			if (Pump < 3.5f || RootCm < 0.1f)
+			{
+				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun pump FAILED: pinned back but the pump is %.2f cm from rest and the root %.2f cm (expected about 4 and a little rack)"), Pump, RootCm);
+			}
+			Shot(TEXT("15b_shotgun_pump"));
+		});
+		Add(SG + 2.5f, TEXT("shotgun pump: pin released, pump home"), [](UWorld*, AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
+		{
+			AGBGun* Gun = ShotgunInHand(Player);
+			if (!Gun)
+			{
+				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun pump: no shotgun in hand"));
+				return;
+			}

+		});
+		Add(SG + 2.6f, TEXT("shotgun enchant: ice enchant on"), [](UWorld*, AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
+		{
+			UGBStatusEffectComponent* Effects = Player->GetStatusEffects();
+			UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun enchant ok=%d"), (Effects && Effects->ApplyEffect(FName(TEXT("Enchant_Ice")), Player)) ? 1 : 0);
+		});
+		Add(SG + 3.2f, TEXT("shotgun weak point: a head hit by the player lights the lens"), [](UWorld* World, AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
+		{
+			const FVector At = Player->GetActorLocation() + Player->GetAimDirection() * 500.f;
+			AGBEnemyCharacter* Enemy = AGBEnemyCharacter::SpawnEnemy(World, FName(TEXT("Orc_Past")), At, FRotator::ZeroRotator, EGBEraPresence::Both);
+			ShotgunFlashTarget = Enemy;
+			if (!Enemy)
+			{
+				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun weakpoint: could not spawn the test orc"));
+				return;
+			}
+			FGBDamageInfo Info;
+			Info.Amount = 1.f;
+			Info.Instigator = Player;
+			Info.bHeadshot = true;
+			UGBCombatLibrary::DealDamage(Enemy, Info);
+		});
+		Add(SG + 3.25f, TEXT("shotgun weak point: flash is on"), [](UWorld*, AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
+		{
+			AGBGun* Gun = ShotgunInHand(Player);
+			if (!Gun)
+			{
+				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun weakpoint: no shotgun in hand"));
+				return;
+			}
+			const float Flash = Gun->GetViewModelWeakPointFlash();
+			UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun weakpoint flash=%.2f"), Flash);
+			if (Flash <= 0.5f)
+			{
+				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun weakpoint FAILED: flash=%.2f right after a weak-point hit (expected more than 0.5)"), Flash);
+			}
+		});
+		Add(SG + 4.3f, TEXT("shotgun weak point: flash is off again, then a body hit"), [](UWorld*, AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
+		{
+			AGBGun* Gun = ShotgunInHand(Player);
+			AGBEnemyCharacter* Enemy = ShotgunFlashTarget.Get();

+			{
+				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun weakpoint: no shotgun in hand or no test orc"));

+			UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun weakpoint flash after=%.2f"), After);
+			if (After > 0.001f)
+			{
+				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun weakpoint FAILED: flash=%.2f one second later (expected 0)"), After);
+			}
+			FGBDamageInfo Info;
+			Info.Amount = 1.f;
+			Info.Instigator = Player;
+			Info.bHeadshot = false;
+			UGBCombatLibrary::DealDamage(Enemy, Info);
+		});
+		Add(SG + 4.4f, TEXT("shotgun body shot: the lens stays dark; half the enchant left"), [](UWorld*, AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
+		{
+			AGBGun* Gun = ShotgunInHand(Player);
+			if (!Gun)
+			{
+				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun bodyshot: no shotgun in hand"));
+				return;
+			}
+			const float Flash = Gun->GetViewModelWeakPointFlash();
+			UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun bodyshot flash=%.2f"), Flash);
+			if (Flash > 0.001f)
+			{
+				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun bodyshot FAILED: flash=%.2f after a body hit (expected 0)"), Flash);
+			}
+			if (AGBEnemyCharacter* Enemy = ShotgunFlashTarget.Get())
+			{
+				Enemy->Destroy();
+			}
+			ShotgunFlashTarget.Reset();
+			if (UGBStatusEffectComponent* Effects = Player->GetStatusEffects())
+			{
+				for (const FGBActiveStatusEffect& Active : Effects->GetActiveEffects())
+				{
+					if (Active.EffectId.ToString().StartsWith(TEXT("Enchant_")))
+					{
+						const FName EnchantId = Active.EffectId;
+						Effects->SetTimeRemaining(EnchantId, 0.5f * Active.TotalDuration);
+						break;
+					}
+				}
+			}
+		});
+		Add(SG + 4.7f, TEXT("shotgun half bar: imbue about half, screenshot"), [](UWorld*, AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
+		{
+			AGBGun* Gun = ShotgunInHand(Player);
+			if (!Gun)
+			{
+				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun half bar: no shotgun in hand"));
+				return;
+			}
+			const float Imbue = Gun->GetViewModelImbueSent();
+			UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun half bar imbue=%.2f ammo=%d"), Imbue, Gun->GetAmmo().InMagazine);
+			CheckEnchantBlink(TEXT("shotgun"), Gun, false, TEXT("at half the enchant"));
+			if (FMath::Abs(Imbue - 0.5f) > 0.05f)
+			{
+				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun half bar FAILED: imbue=%.2f (expected about 0.5)"), Imbue);

+		// Reload from 3 shells with 24 in reserve: one shell per third of a second.
+		const float SR = SG + 4.9f;
+		Add(SR, TEXT("shotgun reload: from 3 shells with 24 in reserve"), [](UWorld* World, AGBPlayerCharacter* Player)
+		{
+			AGBGun* Gun = ShotgunInHand(Player);
+			if (!Gun)
+			{
+				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun reload: no shotgun in hand"));
+				return;
+			}
+			if (UGBStatusEffectComponent* Effects = Player->GetStatusEffects())
+			{
+				Effects->RemoveAllEffects();
+			}
+			FGBAmmoState Ammo;
+			Ammo.InMagazine = 3;
+			Ammo.SpareMagazines = 24;
+			Gun->SetAmmo(Ammo);
+			ShotgunAmmoBefore = Gun->GetAmmo();
+			Gun->Reload();
+			ShotgunReloadStartSim = World->GetTimeSeconds();
+			ShotgunCycleSeconds = Gun->GetStat(GBStat::ReloadTime, 2.f) / FMath::Max(1, Gun->GetMagazineSize());
+			UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun reload started: one shell takes %.3f s;"), ShotgunCycleSeconds);
+			UE_LOG(LogGB, Display, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun reload started: reloading=%d ammo %d / %d"), Gun->IsReloading() ? 1 : 0, Gun->GetAmmo().InMagazine, Gun->GetAmmo().SpareMagazines);
+			if (!Gun->IsReloading() || ShotgunAmmoBefore.InMagazine != 3 || ShotgunAmmoBefore.SpareMagazines != 24)
+			{
+				UE_LOG(LogGB, Error, TEXT("SMOKE shotgun reload FAILED: reloading=%d, ammo %d / %d (expected a reload from 3 with 24 in reserve)"),
+					Gun->IsReloading() ? 1 : 0, ShotgunAmmoBefore.InMagazine, ShotgunAmmoBefore.SpareMagazines);
+			}
+		});

+// ---- Round-by-round reload (the shotgun): pure stepping, FGBPerRoundReload ----
+
+IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBWeaponsPerRoundFullTest, "Glyphbound.Weapons.PerRoundFullReload",
+	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
+bool FGBWeaponsPerRoundFullTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
+{
+	// Empty 6-round gun, 24 in reserve, ReloadTime 2 s: six rounds, done at 2 s and not a cycle earlier or later.
+	int32 Rounds = 0;
+	int32 Reserve = 24;
+	FGBPerRoundReload Reload;
+	Reload.Begin(Rounds, Reserve, 6, 2.f);
+	TestTrue(TEXT("A reload with space and reserve runs"), Reload.bRunning);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("It plans to load the whole magazine"), Reload.Planned, 6);
+
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Just under one cycle moves nothing"), Reload.Advance(2.f / 6.f - 0.01f, Rounds, Reserve, 6), 0);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Nothing is loaded before a cycle ends"), Rounds, 0);
+
+	Reload.Advance(0.02f, Rounds, Reserve, 6);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("The first round lands when its cycle ends"), Rounds, 1);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("It came out of the reserve"), Reserve, 23);
+
+	// Up to just before the end of the reload: five rounds, still running.
+	Reload.Advance(2.f - 2.f / 6.f - 0.02f, Rounds, Reserve, 6);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Five rounds a hair before ReloadTime"), Rounds, 5);
+	TestTrue(TEXT("Still running a hair before ReloadTime"), Reload.bRunning);
+
+	Reload.Advance(0.04f, Rounds, Reserve, 6);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Six rounds at ReloadTime"), Rounds, 6);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Exactly the magazine's worth left the reserve"), Reserve, 18);
+	TestFalse(TEXT("A full gun ends the reload"), Reload.bRunning);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Progress is complete"), Reload.Progress(), 1.f);
+	return true;
+}
+
+IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBWeaponsPerRoundReserveTest, "Glyphbound.Weapons.PerRoundShortReserve",
+	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
+bool FGBWeaponsPerRoundReserveTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
+{
+	// 2 rounds in a 6-round gun but only 1 in reserve: it loads that one, stops, and nothing goes negative.
+	int32 Rounds = 2;
+	int32 Reserve = 1;
+	FGBPerRoundReload Reload;
+	Reload.Begin(Rounds, Reserve, 6, 2.f);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("It plans only what the reserve can give"), Reload.Planned, 1);
+	Reload.Advance(10.f, Rounds, Reserve, 6);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("The one round is loaded"), Rounds, 3);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("The reserve is empty, not negative"), Reserve, 0);
+	TestFalse(TEXT("It stops when the reserve is gone"), Reload.bRunning);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Further time moves nothing"), Reload.Advance(10.f, Rounds, Reserve, 6), 0);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Still 3 rounds"), Rounds, 3);
+
+	// Nothing to load at all: it never runs.
+	int32 FullRounds = 6;
+	int32 FullReserve = 24;
+	FGBPerRoundReload Full;
+	Full.Begin(FullRounds, FullReserve, 6, 2.f);
+	TestFalse(TEXT("A full gun has nothing to load"), Full.bRunning);
+	int32 NoRounds = 0;
+	int32 NoReserve = 0;
+	FGBPerRoundReload Dry;
+	Dry.Begin(NoRounds, NoReserve, 6, 2.f);
+	TestFalse(TEXT("An empty reserve has nothing to load"), Dry.bRunning);
+	return true;
+}
+
+IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBWeaponsPerRoundBigStepTest, "Glyphbound.Weapons.PerRoundLongFrame",
+	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
+bool FGBWeaponsPerRoundBigStepTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
+{
+	// One frame longer than three cycles (3.4 cycles of 1/3 s): three rounds move and the 0.4 cycle is kept.
+	const float Cycle = 2.f / 6.f;
+	int32 Rounds = 0;
+	int32 Reserve = 24;
+	FGBPerRoundReload Reload;
+	Reload.Begin(Rounds, Reserve, 6, 2.f);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Three rounds in one long frame"), Reload.Advance(3.4f * Cycle, Rounds, Reserve, 6), 3);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Gun"), Rounds, 3);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Reserve"), Reserve, 21);
+	TestTrue(TEXT("The remainder of the time is kept"), FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Reload.CycleElapsed, 0.4f * Cycle, 0.001f));
+
+	// The kept 0.4 cycle plus 0.7 more finishes the fourth round (and starts the next one).
+	TestEqual(TEXT("The kept time counts towards the next round"), Reload.Advance(0.7f * Cycle, Rounds, Reserve, 6), 1);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Four rounds"), Rounds, 4);
+	return true;
+}
+
+IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBWeaponsPerRoundStopTest, "Glyphbound.Weapons.PerRoundStopPartWay",
+	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
+bool FGBWeaponsPerRoundStopTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
+{
+	// Two and a half cycles in, the reload is stopped: two rounds stay in the gun, the half-loaded one is not lost.
+	const float Cycle = 2.f / 6.f;
+	int32 Rounds = 1;
+	int32 Reserve = 24;
+	FGBPerRoundReload Reload;
+	Reload.Begin(Rounds, Reserve, 6, 2.f);
+	Reload.Advance(2.5f * Cycle, Rounds, Reserve, 6);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Two rounds moved so far"), Rounds, 3);
+	Reload.Stop();
+	TestFalse(TEXT("Stopped"), Reload.bRunning);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("The rounds already moved stay in the gun"), Rounds, 3);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Only the moved rounds left the reserve; the one in flight is still there"), Reserve, 22);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Rounds in the gun and in the reserve still add up"), Rounds + Reserve, 25);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("A stopped reload moves nothing more"), Reload.Advance(5.f, Rounds, Reserve, 6), 0);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Gun unchanged after the stop"), Rounds, 3);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Reserve unchanged after the stop"), Reserve, 22);
+	return true;
+}

 #endif // WITH_DEV_AUTOMATION_TESTS

+// ---- Round-by-round reload stepping (pure) ----
+
+void FGBPerRoundReload::Begin(int32 Rounds, int32 Reserve, int32 Capacity, float TotalSeconds)
+{
+	CycleSeconds = Capacity > 0 ? FMath::Max(0.f, TotalSeconds) / Capacity : 0.f;
+	CycleElapsed = 0.f;
+	Moved = 0;
+	Planned = FMath::Max(0, FMath::Min(Capacity - Rounds, Reserve));
+	bRunning = Planned > 0;
+}
+
+int32 FGBPerRoundReload::Advance(float DeltaSeconds, int32& Rounds, int32& Reserve, int32 Capacity)
+{
+	if (!bRunning)
+	{
+		return 0;
+	}
+	int32 MovedNow = 0;
+	CycleElapsed += FMath::Max(0.f, DeltaSeconds);
+	// The small tolerance keeps six cycles of 1/3 s from falling a float rounding error short of 2 s.
+	while (bRunning && CycleElapsed + 0.0001f >= CycleSeconds)
+	{
+		CycleElapsed = FMath::Max(0.f, CycleElapsed - CycleSeconds);
+		if (Rounds < Capacity && Reserve > 0)
+		{
+			++Rounds;
+			--Reserve;
+			++Moved;
+			++MovedNow;
+		}
+		if (Rounds >= Capacity || Reserve <= 0)
+		{
+			bRunning = false;
+			CycleElapsed = 0.f;
+		}
+	}
+	return MovedNow;
+}
+
+void FGBPerRoundReload::Stop()
+{
+	bRunning = false;
+	CycleElapsed = 0.f;
+}
+
+float FGBPerRoundReload::Progress() const
+{
+	if (Planned <= 0)
+	{
+		return 1.f;
+	}
+	return FMath::Clamp((Moved + CycleProgress()) / Planned, 0.f, 1.f);
+}
+
+float FGBPerRoundReload::CycleProgress() const
+{
+	return (bRunning && CycleSeconds > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER) ? FMath::Clamp(CycleElapsed / CycleSeconds, 0.f, 1.f) : 0.f;
+}

+bool AGBGun::ReloadsPerRound() const

+}

+void AGBGun::SetPumpPreview(float Amount)

+	{
