+	}
+}
+
+void AGBGun::ClearPumpPreview()
+{
+	PumpPreview = -1.f;
+	PumpElapsed = -1.f;
+	if (bViewModel)
+	{
+		ApplyViewModelPose();
+	}
+}
+
+void AGBGun::StartPump()
+{
+	if (ModelArt && ModelArt->bPump && !bReloading)
+	{
+		PumpElapsed = 0.f;
+	}
+}
+
+void AGBGun::ResetPump()
+{
+	PumpElapsed = -1.f;
+	PumpPreview = -1.f;
+	if (bViewModel)
+	{
+		ApplyPump();
+	}
+}
+
+void AGBGun::ApplyPump()
+{
+	float Amount = 0.f;
+	if (ModelArt && ModelArt->bPump)
+	{
+		if (PumpPreview >= 0.f)
+		{
+			Amount = PumpPreview;
+		}
+		else if (PumpElapsed >= 0.f)
+		{
+			// Shares of the current FireInterval: still, back, fully back, home, still.
+			const float T = PumpElapsed / FMath::Max(0.05f, GetStat(GBStat::FireInterval, 0.5f));
+			if (T >= PumpStartShare && T < PumpBackShare)
+			{
+				Amount = Smoothstep01(Share(T, PumpStartShare, PumpBackShare));
+			}
+			else if (T >= PumpBackShare && T < PumpHomeShare)
+			{
+				Amount = 1.f - Smoothstep01(Share(T, PumpBackShare, PumpHomeShare));
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	CurrentPumpAmount = Amount;
+	if (VMPump && ModelArt)
+	{
+		VMPump->SetRelativeLocation(ModelArt->PumpRest + ModelArt->PumpSlide * Amount);
+	}
+}
+
 float AGBGun::GetReloadAnimProgress() const
 {
 	if (ReloadPreview >= 0.f)
 	{
 		return ReloadPreview;
 	}
-	return bReloading ? GetReloadProgress() : -1.f;
+	if (!bReloading)
+	{
+		return -1.f;
+	}
+	return ReloadsPerRound() ? PerRound.CycleProgress() : GetReloadProgress();
+}
+
+FGBReloadLook AGBGun::ComputeReloadLook() const
+{
+	FGBReloadLook Look;
+	const float Progress = GetReloadAnimProgress();
+	if (Progress < 0.f)
+	{
+		return Look;
+	}
+	Look.bActive = true;
+	if (!ReloadsPerRound())
+	{
+		// Magazine reload: slide out along MagazineOut, (fall), (rise), slide in; the slide share is 1 while the magazine is away.
+		Look.Pose = GBReloadAnim::PoseAmount(Progress);
+		Look.Dip = ReloadDip(Progress);
+		Look.Bump = ReloadBump(Progress);
+		Look.Drop = ReloadDrop(Progress);
+		Look.Slide = 1.f;
+		if (Progress < ReloadSlideOutEnd)
+		{
+			Look.Slide = Smoothstep01(Share(Progress, ReloadTiltEnd, ReloadSlideOutEnd));
+		}
+		else if (Progress >= ReloadRiseEnd)
+		{
+			Look.Slide = 1.f - FMath::Pow(Share(Progress, ReloadRiseEnd, ReloadInEnd), ReloadSlideInEase);
+		}
+		Look.bShowMag = Progress < ReloadOutEnd || Progress >= ReloadEmptyEnd;
+		return Look;
+	}
+	// One round of a round-by-round reload: the "fresh magazine" beats of the magazine guns, once per round.
+	// The pose eases in during the first round only (a pinned preview holds it fully); the last ease-out is the blend after the end.
+	const bool bFirstRound = ReloadPreview < 0.f && PerRound.Moved == 0;
+	Look.Pose = bFirstRound ? Smoothstep01(Progress / PerRoundPoseInShare) : 1.f;
+	const float Beat = FMath::Lerp(ReloadEmptyEnd, ReloadSeatEnd, Progress);
+	Look.Bump = ReloadBump(Beat);
+	Look.Drop = ReloadDrop(Beat);
+	Look.Slide = Beat < ReloadRiseEnd ? 1.f : 1.f - FMath::Pow(Share(Beat, ReloadRiseEnd, ReloadInEnd), ReloadSlideInEase);
+	Look.bShowMag = Progress < 0.98f; // seated: inside the port, the next round starts below the view
+	return Look;
+}
+
+GBReloadAnim::FHandBeat AGBGun::GetReloadHandBeat() const
+{
+	GBReloadAnim::FHandBeat Beat;
+	if (!bViewModel)
+	{
+		return Beat;
+	}
+	const FGBReloadLook Look = ComputeReloadLook();
+	if (Look.bActive)
+	{
+		Beat.Reach = Look.Pose;
+		Beat.Drop = Look.Drop;
+		Beat.Push = Look.Bump;
+	}
+	else if (ReloadsPerRound())
+	{
+		Beat.Reach = CurrentPoseAmount;
+	}
+	return Beat;
+}
+
+float AGBGun::GetReloadPoseAmount() const
+{
+	if (!bViewModel)
+	{
+		return 0.f;
+	}
+	const FGBReloadLook Look = ComputeReloadLook();
+	return Look.bActive ? Look.Pose : (ReloadsPerRound() ? CurrentPoseAmount : 0.f);
 }

+/** What the reload animation shows this frame: the same record for a magazine reload and for one round of a round-by-round reload. */
+struct FGBReloadLook
+{
+	bool bActive = false;
+	float Pose = 0.f;   // 0..1 how far the gun is in its reload pose
+	float Dip = 0.f;    // 0..1 the dip as a magazine leaves
+	float Bump = 0.f;   // 0..1 the seat bump
+	float Drop = 0.f;   // 0..1 how far down the magazine / round (and the left hand) is
+	float Slide = 0.f;  // 0..1 along the slide path (1 = fully out)
+	bool bShowMag = true;
+};
+
+/**
+ * Pure stepping of a round-by-round reload (the shotgun): no world, no actor, tested in Tests/GBWeaponsTests.cpp.
+ * One round takes CycleSeconds (the gun's reload time over its magazine size). The round moves from the reserve into
+ * the gun only when its cycle ENDS, so a reload stopped part-way loses nothing: the round in flight is still in the
+ * reserve. When a cycle ends and the gun is not full and the reserve is not empty, the next cycle starts at once.
+ */
+struct FGBPerRoundReload
+{
+	float CycleSeconds = 0.f;
+	/** Seconds into the current cycle. */
+	float CycleElapsed = 0.f;
+	/** Rounds moved so far. */
+	int32 Moved = 0;
+	/** Rounds this reload set out to load (the smaller of the empty space and the reserve). */
+	int32 Planned = 0;
+	bool bRunning = false;
+
+	/** Starts a reload; one round takes TotalSeconds / Capacity. Not running at all when there is nothing to load. */
+	void Begin(int32 Rounds, int32 Reserve, int32 Capacity, float TotalSeconds);
+	/** Adds time and moves every round whose cycle ended (a long step moves several, none of the time is lost). Returns how many moved. */
+	int32 Advance(float DeltaSeconds, int32& Rounds, int32& Reserve, int32 Capacity);
+	/** Stops part-way: the rounds already moved stay in the gun, the round in flight stays in the reserve. */
+	void Stop();
+	/** 0 to 1 over the whole reload (the rounds it set out to load). */
+	float Progress() const;
+	/** 0 to 1 inside the current round's cycle. */
+	float CycleProgress() const;
+};
+

  26: // Every vector is in cm in the body's unscaled frame (source in metres, 1 unit = 1 cm); the view scale applies to the body.

  48: 	FVector ReloadOffset;         // reload pose: cm added to the root (camera frame: x forward, y right, z up) at full pose
  49: 	FRotator ReloadRot;           // reload pose: degrees added to the root; roll cants the magazine well towards the camera
  50: 	float ReloadFallCm;           // how far (cm, camera down) the old magazine falls before it is below the view; the new one rises from there
  51: 	float ReloadDipCm;            // the gun dips this much as the magazine leaves

 110: 	{

 112: 		{
 113: 			if (Name == Art->Name)
 114: 			{

 116: 			}
 117: 		}
 118: 		return nullptr;
 119: 	}

 121: 	// Trigger pull animation total time in seconds (linear in, linear out).
 122: 	constexpr float TriggerPullSeconds = 0.12f;
 123: 	// Trigger pitch at full pull in degrees (the bottom of the trigger moves back towards the grip).
 124: 	constexpr float TriggerPullPitch = -8.f;
 125: 	// Material parameters set by the game on every model slot.
 126: 	const FName ElementColorParam(TEXT("ElementColor"));
 127: 	const FName ImbueRemainingParam(TEXT("ImbueRemaining"));
 128: 	const FName FlashParam(TEXT("Flash"));
 129: 	const FName DisplayTextureParam(TEXT("DisplayTexture"));
 130: 	// Weak-point light: full glow for PistolWeakPointFlashHold seconds after a weak-point hit, then a linear fade over
 131: 	// PistolWeakPointFlashFade seconds (Game.json; halved by the owner, GDD v0.30). The key names stay, both guns use them.
 132: 	const FName WeakPointFlashHoldKey(TEXT("PistolWeakPointFlashHold"));
 133: 	const FName WeakPointFlashFadeKey(TEXT("PistolWeakPointFlashFade"));
 134: 	// Energy bar: it blinks while the enchant has GunEnchantBlinkSeconds or less left, GunEnchantBlinkHz times per
 135: 	// second (Game.json; owner: "the light that shows the enchant's time starts to blink when 3 seconds are left").
 136: 	const FName EnchantBlinkSecondsKey(TEXT("GunEnchantBlinkSeconds"));
 137: 	const FName EnchantBlinkHzKey(TEXT("GunEnchantBlinkHz"));
 138: 	// Holographic ammo display: render target size in pixels. Provisional, owner's note 1.
 139: 	constexpr int32 AmmoDisplayWidth = 256;
 140: 	constexpr int32 AmmoDisplayHeight = 128;
 141: 	const TCHAR* const AmmoDisplayReloadText = TEXT("--");
 142: 	// Frame drawn by the game on a plain display (FGBGunModelArt::bDrawDisplayFrame), as shares of the display from its
 143: 	// top left. Copied from the look of the pistol's rear screen: a top and a bottom line, a status bar on each side,
 144: 	// and a row of pips under the digits, one per round, lit while that round is in the magazine. Provisional.
 145: 	constexpr int32 AmmoDisplayMaxPips = 30;        // larger magazines share pips (2 rounds per pip, 3...)
 146: 	constexpr float AmmoDisplaySpentPip = 0.16f;    // brightness of a spent round's pip
 147: 	constexpr float AmmoDisplayPipFill = 0.68f;     // lit share of each pip's slot; the rest is the gap
 148: 	struct FGBDisplayBox { float X0, Y0, X1, Y1; };
 149: 	const FGBDisplayBox AmmoDisplayFrame[] = {
 150: 		{ 0.10f, 0.095f, 0.78f, 0.125f },   // top line
 151: 		{ 0.08f, 0.885f, 0.60f, 0.915f },   // bottom line
 152: 		{ 0.075f, 0.26f, 0.10f, 0.60f },    // left status bar
 153: 		{ 0.90f, 0.26f, 0.925f, 0.60f } };  // right status bar
 154: 	const FGBDisplayBox AmmoDisplayPipRow = { 0.075f, 0.70f, 0.925f, 0.80f };
 155: 	// Shot kick: the root eases back to rest over this time (ease out). Provisional.
 156: 	constexpr float KickSeconds = 0.09f;
 157: 	// Muzzle flash: visible this long, two crossed thin slabs of this size (cm), a random roll and 85-120 % scale.
 158: 	constexpr float MuzzleFlashSeconds = 0.05f;
 159: 	constexpr float MuzzleFlashSizeCm = 7.f;
 160: 	constexpr float MuzzleFlashThicknessCm = 0.3f;
 161: 	// Brightness multiplier of the flash colour (the glow material is additive).
 162: 	constexpr float MuzzleFlashBrightness = 2.f;
 163: 	// Visual timers never advance by more than this per frame, so a hitch cannot skip a kick or a flash entirely.
 164: 	constexpr float MaxEffectStep = 1.f / 30.f;
 165: 
 166: 	// Reload animation: shares of the reload (GetReloadAnimProgress, 0 to 1). Provisional art values.
 167: 	constexpr float ReloadTiltEnd = 0.15f;       // the gun leaves rest for the reload pose
 168: 	constexpr float ReloadDipEnd = 0.35f;        // the dip as the magazine leaves is over
 169: 	constexpr float ReloadSlideOutEnd = 0.25f;   // the old magazine has slid out along MagazineOut, then it falls
 170: 	constexpr float ReloadOutEnd = 0.40f;        // the old magazine is below the view and hidden
 171: 	constexpr float ReloadEmptyEnd = 0.50f;      // empty well; a fresh magazine appears below the view
 172: 	constexpr float ReloadRiseEnd = 0.62f;       // the fresh magazine has risen to the end of the slide path
 173: 	constexpr float ReloadInEnd = 0.80f;         // the fresh magazine is seated
 174: 	constexpr float ReloadSeatEnd = 0.86f;       // the seat bump is over; the gun eases back to rest
 175: 	constexpr float ReloadSlideInEase = 1.6f;    // exponent of the slide-in (above 1: fastest at the end)
 176: 	// A cancelled reload (or a released preview) blends the gun back to rest over this time.
 177: 	constexpr float ReloadBlendSeconds = 0.12f;
 178: 	// Round-by-round reload (look): the gun leaves rest during this share of the FIRST round's cycle, then holds the pose.
 179: 	// Each round's cycle plays the magazine guns' "fresh magazine" beats (rise, slide in, seat bump) from ReloadEmptyEnd to ReloadSeatEnd.
 180: 	constexpr float PerRoundPoseInShare = 0.35f;
 181: 	// After the last round the gun eases back to rest over this time (it may run past the gameplay end of the reload).
 182: 	constexpr float PerRoundExitSeconds = 0.22f;
 183: 	// Pump: shares of the gun's FireInterval. Provisional.
 184: 	constexpr float PumpStartShare = 0.22f;      // the pump starts to slide back
 185: 	constexpr float PumpBackShare = 0.45f;       // fully back
 186: 	constexpr float PumpHomeShare = 0.72f;       // home again, well before the next shot is allowed
 187: 
 188: 	float Smoothstep01(float T)
 189: 	{
 190: 		T = FMath::Clamp(T, 0.f, 1.f);
 191: 		return T * T * (3.f - 2.f * T);
 192: 	}
 193: 
 194: 	// Share of [From, To] that Progress has covered, clamped to 0..1.
 195: 	float Share(float Progress, float From, float To)
 196: 	{
 197: 		return FMath::Clamp((Progress - From) / (To - From), 0.f, 1.f);
 198: 	}
 199: 
 200: 	// 0..1: dip as the magazine leaves, and bump as the new one seats (half sine over their beats).
 201: 	float ReloadDip(float Progress)
 202: 	{
 203: 		return (Progress > ReloadTiltEnd && Progress < ReloadDipEnd) ? FMath::Sin(PI * Share(Progress, ReloadTiltEnd, ReloadDipEnd)) : 0.f;
 204: 	}
 205: 	float ReloadBump(float Progress)
 206: 	{
 207: 		return (Progress > ReloadInEnd && Progress < ReloadSeatEnd) ? FMath::Sin(PI * Share(Progress, ReloadInEnd, ReloadSeatEnd)) : 0.f;
 208: 	}
 209: 	// 0..1 how far down the magazine (and the left hand) is: falls with t^2 after the slide, rises with (1-t)^2 after the empty beat.
 210: 	float ReloadDrop(float Progress)
 211: 	{
 212: 		if (Progress < ReloadSlideOutEnd)
 213: 		{
 214: 			return 0.f;
 215: 		}
 216: 		if (Progress < ReloadOutEnd)
 217: 		{
 218: 			const float Fall = Share(Progress, ReloadSlideOutEnd, ReloadOutEnd);
 219: 			return Fall * Fall;
 220: 		}
 221: 		if (Progress < ReloadEmptyEnd)
 222: 		{
 223: 			return 1.f;
 224: 		}
 225: 		const float Rise = 1.f - Share(Progress, ReloadEmptyEnd, ReloadRiseEnd);
 226: 		return Rise * Rise;
 227: 	}
 228: }
 229: 
 230: namespace GBReloadAnim
 231: {
 232: 	float PoseAmount(float Progress)
 233: 	{
 234: 		if (Progress < 0.f)
 235: 		{
 236: 			return 0.f;
 237: 		}
 238: 		if (Progress < ReloadSeatEnd)
 239: 		{
 240: 			return Smoothstep01(Progress / ReloadTiltEnd);
 241: 		}
 242: 		return 1.f - Smoothstep01(Share(Progress, ReloadSeatEnd, 1.f));
 243: 	}
 244: 
 245: 	FHandBeat HandBeat(float Progress)
 246: 	{
 247: 		FHandBeat Beat;
 248: 		if (Progress >= 0.f)
 249: 		{
 250: 			Beat.Reach = PoseAmount(Progress);
 251: 			// The hand starts to fall only once the magazine has slid out, but it already reaches in during the tilt.
 252: 			Beat.Drop = ReloadDrop(Progress);
 253: 			Beat.Push = ReloadBump(Progress);
 254: 		}
 255: 		return Beat;
 256: 	}
 257: }
 258: 
 259: // ---- Round-by-round reload stepping (pure) ----
 260: 
 261: void FGBPerRoundReload::Begin(int32 Rounds, int32 Reserve, int32 Capacity, float TotalSeconds)
 262: {
 263: 	CycleSeconds = Capacity > 0 ? FMath::Max(0.f, TotalSeconds) / Capacity : 0.f;
 264: 	CycleElapsed = 0.f;
 265: 	Moved = 0;
 266: 	Planned = FMath::Max(0, FMath::Min(Capacity - Rounds, Reserve));
 267: 	bRunning = Planned > 0;
 268: }
 269: 
 270: int32 FGBPerRoundReload::Advance(float DeltaSeconds, int32& Rounds, int32& Reserve, int32 Capacity)
 271: {
 272: 	if (!bRunning)
 273: 	{
 274: 		return 0;
 275: 	}
 276: 	int32 MovedNow = 0;
 277: 	CycleElapsed += FMath::Max(0.f, DeltaSeconds);
 278: 	// The small tolerance keeps six cycles of 1/3 s from falling a float rounding error short of 2 s.
 279: 	while (bRunning && CycleElapsed + 0.0001f >= CycleSeconds)
 280: 	{
 281: 		CycleElapsed = FMath::Max(0.f, CycleElapsed - CycleSeconds);
 282: 		if (Rounds < Capacity && Reserve > 0)
 283: 		{
 284: 			++Rounds;
 285: 			--Reserve;
 286: 			++Moved;
 287: 			++MovedNow;
 288: 		}
 289: 		if (Rounds >= Capacity || Reserve <= 0)
 290: 		{
 291: 			bRunning = false;
 292: 			CycleElapsed = 0.f;
 293: 		}
 294: 	}
 295: 	return MovedNow;
 296: }
 297: 
 298: void FGBPerRoundReload::Stop()
 299: {
 300: 	bRunning = false;
 301: 	CycleElapsed = 0.f;
 302: }
 303: 
 304: float FGBPerRoundReload::Progress() const
 305: {
 306: 	if (Planned <= 0)
 307: 	{
 308: 		return 1.f;
 309: 	}
 310: 	return FMath::Clamp((Moved + CycleProgress()) / Planned, 0.f, 1.f);
 311: }
 312: 
 313: float FGBPerRoundReload::CycleProgress() const
 314: {
 315: 	return (bRunning && CycleSeconds > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER) ? FMath::Clamp(CycleElapsed / CycleSeconds, 0.f, 1.f) : 0.f;
 316: }

 363: 		{
 364: 			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Weapons.json '%s': unknown ViewModel '%s' (Pistol, Rifle or Shotgun); using the placeholder shapes instead."),

 366: 		}

 369: }

 623: {
 624: 	PumpElapsed = -1.f;
 625: 	PumpPreview = -1.f;

 629: 	}

 631: 
 632: void AGBGun::ApplyPump()
 633: {
 634: 	float Amount = 0.f;
 635: 	if (ModelArt && ModelArt->bPump)
 636: 	{
 637: 		if (PumpPreview >= 0.f)
 638: 		{
 639: 			Amount = PumpPreview;
 640: 		}
 641: 		else if (PumpElapsed >= 0.f)
 642: 		{
 643: 			// Shares of the current FireInterval: still, back, fully back, home, still.
 644: 			const float T = PumpElapsed / FMath::Max(0.05f, GetStat(GBStat::FireInterval, 0.5f));
 645: 			if (T >= PumpStartShare && T < PumpBackShare)
 646: 			{
 647: 				Amount = Smoothstep01(Share(T, PumpStartShare, PumpBackShare));
 648: 			}
 649: 			else if (T >= PumpBackShare && T < PumpHomeShare)
 650: 			{
 651: 				Amount = 1.f - Smoothstep01(Share(T, PumpBackShare, PumpHomeShare));
 652: 			}
 653: 		}
 654: 	}
 655: 	CurrentPumpAmount = Amount;
 656: 	if (VMPump && ModelArt)
 657: 	{
 658: 		VMPump->SetRelativeLocation(ModelArt->PumpRest + ModelArt->PumpSlide * Amount);
 659: 	}
 660: }
 661: 
 662: float AGBGun::GetReloadAnimProgress() const
 663: {
 664: 	if (ReloadPreview >= 0.f)
 665: 	{
 666: 		return ReloadPreview;
 667: 	}
 668: 	if (!bReloading)
 669: 	{
 670: 		return -1.f;
 671: 	}
 672: 	return ReloadsPerRound() ? PerRound.CycleProgress() : GetReloadProgress();
 673: }
 674: 
 675: FGBReloadLook AGBGun::ComputeReloadLook() const
 676: {
 677: 	FGBReloadLook Look;
 678: 	const float Progress = GetReloadAnimProgress();
 679: 	if (Progress < 0.f)
 680: 	{
 681: 		return Look;
 682: 	}
 683: 	Look.bActive = true;
 684: 	if (!ReloadsPerRound())
 685: 	{
 686: 		// Magazine reload: slide out along MagazineOut, (fall), (rise), slide in; the slide share is 1 while the magazine is away.
 687: 		Look.Pose = GBReloadAnim::PoseAmount(Progress);
 688: 		Look.Dip = ReloadDip(Progress);
 689: 		Look.Bump = ReloadBump(Progress);
 690: 		Look.Drop = ReloadDrop(Progress);
 691: 		Look.Slide = 1.f;
 692: 		if (Progress < ReloadSlideOutEnd)
 693: 		{
 694: 			Look.Slide = Smoothstep01(Share(Progress, ReloadTiltEnd, ReloadSlideOutEnd));
 695: 		}
 696: 		else if (Progress >= ReloadRiseEnd)
 697: 		{
 698: 			Look.Slide = 1.f - FMath::Pow(Share(Progress, ReloadRiseEnd, ReloadInEnd), ReloadSlideInEase);
 699: 		}
 700: 		Look.bShowMag = Progress < ReloadOutEnd || Progress >= ReloadEmptyEnd;
 701: 		return Look;
 702: 	}
 703: 	// One round of a round-by-round reload: the "fresh magazine" beats of the magazine guns, once per round.
 704: 	// The pose eases in during the first round only (a pinned preview holds it fully); the last ease-out is the blend after the end.
 705: 	const bool bFirstRound = ReloadPreview < 0.f && PerRound.Moved == 0;
 706: 	Look.Pose = bFirstRound ? Smoothstep01(Progress / PerRoundPoseInShare) : 1.f;
 707: 	const float Beat = FMath::Lerp(ReloadEmptyEnd, ReloadSeatEnd, Progress);
 708: 	Look.Bump = ReloadBump(Beat);
 709: 	Look.Drop = ReloadDrop(Beat);
 710: 	Look.Slide = Beat < ReloadRiseEnd ? 1.f : 1.f - FMath::Pow(Share(Beat, ReloadRiseEnd, ReloadInEnd), ReloadSlideInEase);
 711: 	Look.bShowMag = Progress < 0.98f; // seated: inside the port, the next round starts below the view
 712: 	return Look;
 713: }
 714: 
 715: GBReloadAnim::FHandBeat AGBGun::GetReloadHandBeat() const
 716: {
 717: 	GBReloadAnim::FHandBeat Beat;
 718: 	if (!bViewModel)
 719: 	{
 720: 		return Beat;
 721: 	}
 722: 	const FGBReloadLook Look = ComputeReloadLook();
 723: 	if (Look.bActive)
 724: 	{
 725: 		Beat.Reach = Look.Pose;
 726: 		Beat.Drop = Look.Drop;
 727: 		Beat.Push = Look.Bump;
 728: 	}
 729: 	else if (ReloadsPerRound())
 730: 	{
 731: 		Beat.Reach = CurrentPoseAmount;
 732: 	}
 733: 	return Beat;
 734: }
 735: 
 736: float AGBGun::GetReloadPoseAmount() const
 737: {
 738: 	if (!bViewModel)
 739: 	{
 740: 		return 0.f;
 741: 	}
 742: 	const FGBReloadLook Look = ComputeReloadLook();
 743: 	return Look.bActive ? Look.Pose : (ReloadsPerRound() ? CurrentPoseAmount : 0.f);
 744: }

 746: void AGBGun::SetReloadPreview(float Progress)
 747: {
 748: 	ReloadPreview = FMath::Clamp(Progress, 0.f, 1.f);
 749: 	ReloadBlendElapsed = -1.f;
 750: 	if (bViewModel)

 752: 		ApplyViewModelPose();
 753: 		ApplyReloadMagazine();
 754: 	}
 755: }
 756: 
 757: void AGBGun::ClearReloadPreview()
 758: {
 759: 	if (ReloadPreview < 0.f)
 760: 	{
 761: 		return;
 762: 	}
 763: 	if (bViewModel && !bReloading)
 764: 	{
 765: 		BeginReloadBlend(ReloadBlendSeconds);
 766: 	}
 767: 	ReloadPreview = -1.f;
 768: 	if (bViewModel)
 769: 	{
 770: 		ApplyViewModelPose();
 771: 		ApplyReloadMagazine();
 772: 	}
 773: }
 774: 
 775: void AGBGun::BeginReloadBlend(float Seconds)
 776: {
 777: 	if (!bViewModel)
 778: 	{
 779: 		return;
 780: 	}
 781: 	ReloadBlendLength = FMath::Max(0.01f, Seconds);
 782: 	ReloadBlendLoc = ReloadPoseLoc;
 783: 	ReloadBlendRot = ReloadPoseRot;
 784: 	ReloadBlendElapsed = 0.f;
 785: }
 786: 
 787: void AGBGun::ResetReloadVisuals()
 788: {
 789: 	ReloadPreview = -1.f;
 790: 	ReloadBlendElapsed = -1.f;
 791: 	ReloadPoseLoc = FVector::ZeroVector;
 792: 	ReloadPoseRot = FRotator::ZeroRotator;
 793: 	CurrentPoseAmount = 0.f;
 794: 	ResetPump();
 795: 	if (bViewModel)
 796: 	{
 797: 		ApplyViewModelPose();
 798: 		ApplyReloadMagazine();
 799: 	}
 800: }
 801: 
 802: void AGBGun::ApplyViewModelPose()
 803: {
 804: 	if (!ModelArt)
 805: 	{
 806: 		return;
 807: 	}
 808: 	const FGBGunModelArt& Art = *ModelArt;
 809: 	// Shot kick, ease out: the push is full at the shot and shrinks as (1 - t)^2 over KickSeconds.
 810: 	float Strength = 0.f;
 811: 	if (KickElapsed >= 0.f)
 812: 	{
 813: 		const float Remaining = 1.f - FMath::Clamp(KickElapsed / KickSeconds, 0.f, 1.f);
 814: 		Strength = Remaining * Remaining;
 815: 	}
 816: 	CurrentKickBackCm = Art.KickBackCm * Strength;
 817: 
 818: 	// Pump first: its amount drives the small rack motion of the root.
 819: 	ApplyPump();
 820: 
 821: 	// Reload pose: rest -> reload pose -> rest, with a dip as the magazine leaves and a bump as the new one seats.
 822: 	// After a cancel the pose it had blends to rest instead of snapping.
 823: 	const FGBReloadLook Look = ComputeReloadLook();
 824: 	if (Look.bActive)
 825: 	{
 826: 		ReloadPoseLoc = Art.ReloadOffset * Look.Pose + FVector(0.f, 0.f, Art.ReloadBumpCm * Look.Bump - Art.ReloadDipCm * Look.Dip);
 827: 		ReloadPoseRot = Art.ReloadRot * Look.Pose;
 828: 		CurrentPoseAmount = Look.Pose;
 829: 	}
 830: 	else if (ReloadBlendElapsed >= 0.f)
 831: 	{
 832: 		const float Left = 1.f - Smoothstep01(ReloadBlendElapsed / ReloadBlendLength);
 833: 		ReloadPoseLoc = ReloadBlendLoc * Left;
 834: 		ReloadPoseRot = ReloadBlendRot * Left;
 835: 		CurrentPoseAmount = Left;
 836: 	}
 837: 	else
 838: 	{
 839: 		ReloadPoseLoc = FVector::ZeroVector;
 840: 		ReloadPoseRot = FRotator::ZeroRotator;
 841: 		CurrentPoseAmount = 0.f;
 842: 	}
 843: 
 844: 	// The pump's rack: the gun dips and rolls a little while the pump is back. It adds to the shot kick.
 845: 	const FVector RackLoc(0.f, 0.f, -Art.PumpRackDipCm * CurrentPumpAmount);
 846: 	const FRotator RackRot(0.f, 0.f, Art.PumpRackRollDeg * CurrentPumpAmount);
 847: 	Root->SetRelativeLocation(Art.RootOffset + FVector(-CurrentKickBackCm, 0.f, 0.f) + ReloadPoseLoc + RackLoc);
 848: 	Root->SetRelativeRotation(FRotator(Art.KickPitchDeg * Strength, Art.RootYaw, 0.f) + ReloadPoseRot + RackRot);
 849: }
 850: 
1190: 	AddPart(TEXT("Body"), EGBShape::Cube, FVector(BodyForward, 0.f, 0.f), FVector(BodyLength, 5.f, 7.5f), BodyColor, false);
1191: 	AddPart(TEXT("Barrel"), EGBShape::Cube, FVector(BodyForward + BodyLength * 0.5f + BarrelLength * 0.5f, 0.f, 1.5f),
1192: 		FVector(BarrelLength, BarrelThickness, BarrelThickness), BarrelColor, false);
1193: 	AddPart(TEXT("Grip"), EGBShape::Cube, FVector(BodyForward - BodyLength * 0.5f + 2.5f, 0.f, -7.f), FVector(4.f, 4.f, 9.f), GripColor, false);
1194: 	if (bAutomaticLook)
1195: 	{
1196: 		AddPart(TEXT("Magazine"), EGBShape::Cube, FVector(BodyForward + 2.f, 0.f, -7.f), FVector(3.f, 2.5f, 8.f), BodyColor, false);
1197: 	}
1198: 	// Neon on the camera-facing side (the anchor sits right of the view, so +Y faces away).
1199: 	AddPart(TEXT("Neon"), EGBShape::Cube, FVector(BodyForward, -2.6f, 1.f), FVector(BodyLength * 0.8f, 0.35f, 0.7f), NeonColor, true);
1200: 	// Angle the whole viewmodel slightly toward the view center so the barrel points near the crosshair.
1201: 	Root->SetRelativeRotation(FRotator(0.f, -6.f, 0.f));
1202: }
1203: 
1204: // ---- Ammo ----
1205: 
1206: int32 AGBGun::GetMagazineSize() const
1207: {
1208: 	return FMath::Max(0, FMath::RoundToInt(GetStat(GBStat::MagazineSize, 1.f)));
1209: }
1210: 
1211: int32 AGBGun::GetMaxSpareMagazines() const
1212: {
1213: 	const int32 Magazines = FMath::Max(0, FMath::RoundToInt(GetStat(GBStat::SpareMagazines, 0.f)));
1214: 	// A round-by-round gun keeps its reserve in rounds: the same total the magazines would have held.
1215: 	return ReloadsPerRound() ? Magazines * GetMagazineSize() : Magazines;
1216: }
1217: 
1218: void AGBGun::SetAmmo(const FGBAmmoState& InAmmo)
1219: {
1220: 	CancelReload();
1221: 	Ammo.InMagazine = FMath::Clamp(InAmmo.InMagazine, 0, GetMagazineSize());
1222: 	Ammo.SpareMagazines = FMath::Clamp(InAmmo.SpareMagazines, 0, GetMaxSpareMagazines());
1223: 	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
1224: }
1225: 
1226: void AGBGun::RefillAmmo()
1227: {
1228: 	CancelReload();
1229: 	Ammo.InMagazine = GetMagazineSize();
1230: 	Ammo.SpareMagazines = GetMaxSpareMagazines();
1231: 	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
1232: }
1233: 
1234: int32 AGBGun::AddSpareMagazines(int32 Count)
1235: {
1236: 	if (Count <= 0)
1237: 	{
1238: 		return 0;
1239: 	}
1240: 	if (ReloadsPerRound())
1241: 	{
1242: 		// Count magazines' worth of rounds; the result is how many magazines' worth went in, rounded up.
1243: 		const int32 PerMagazine = FMath::Max(1, GetMagazineSize());
1244: 		const int32 AddedRounds = FMath::Clamp(GetMaxSpareMagazines() - Ammo.SpareMagazines, 0, Count * PerMagazine);
1245: 		if (AddedRounds > 0)
1246: 		{
1247: 			Ammo.SpareMagazines += AddedRounds;
1248: 			NotifyEquipmentChanged();
1249: 		}
1250: 		return FMath::DivideAndRoundUp(AddedRounds, PerMagazine);
1251: 	}
1252: 	const int32 Added = FMath::Clamp(GetMaxSpareMagazines() - Ammo.SpareMagazines, 0, Count);
1253: 	if (Added > 0)
1254: 	{
1255: 		Ammo.SpareMagazines += Added;
1256: 		NotifyEquipmentChanged();
1257: 	}
1258: 	return Added;
1259: }
1260: 
1261: // ---- Reload ----
1262: 
1263: void AGBGun::Reload()
1264: {
1265: 	if (bReloading || !bEquipped)
1266: 	{
1267: 		return;
1268: 	}
1269: 	if (Ammo.InMagazine >= GetMagazineSize() || Ammo.SpareMagazines <= 0)
1270: 	{
1271: 		return;
1272: 	}
1273: 	bReloading = true;
1274: 	ReloadElapsed = 0.f;
1275: 	ReloadDuration = FMath::Max(0.f, GetStat(GBStat::ReloadTime, 1.f));
1276: 	ResetPump();
1277: 	if (ReloadsPerRound())
1278: 	{
1279: 		PerRound.Begin(Ammo.InMagazine, Ammo.SpareMagazines, GetMagazineSize(), ReloadDuration);
1280: 	}
1281: 	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
1282: }
1283: 
1284: void AGBGun::CancelReload()
1285: {
1286: 	if (!bReloading)
1287: 	{
1288: 		return;
1289: 	}
1290: 	// Blend from the pose the gun is in now (the pinned preview, if any, keeps owning the pose until it is released).
1291: 	if (ReloadPreview < 0.f)
1292: 	{
1293: 		BeginReloadBlend(ReloadBlendSeconds);
1294: 	}
1295: 	bReloading = false;
1296: 	ReloadElapsed = 0.f;
1297: 	PerRound.Stop(); // rounds already loaded stay loaded; the round in flight was never taken from the reserve
1298: 	bFireWhenLoaded = false;
1299: 	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
1300: }
1301: 
1302: void AGBGun::FinishPerRoundReload()
1303: {
1304: 	bReloading = false;
1305: 	ReloadElapsed = 0.f;
1306: 	// The gun eases back to rest a little slower than a cancel and may still be moving when it is allowed to fire.
1307: 	if (ReloadPreview < 0.f)
1308: 	{
1309: 		BeginReloadBlend(PerRoundExitSeconds);
1310: 	}
1311: 	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
1312: }
1313: 
1314: void AGBGun::FinishReload()
1315: {
1316: 	bReloading = false;
1317: 	ReloadElapsed = 0.f;
1318: 	if (Ammo.SpareMagazines > 0)
1319: 	{
1320: 		// The rounds left in the old magazine are lost (provisional, classic tactical style).
1321: 		--Ammo.SpareMagazines;
1322: 		Ammo.InMagazine = GetMagazineSize();
1323: 	}
1324: 	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
1325: }
1326: 
1327: float AGBGun::GetReloadProgress() const
1328: {
1329: 	if (!bReloading)
1330: 	{
1331: 		return 0.f;
1332: 	}
1333: 	if (ReloadsPerRound())
1334: 	{
1335: 		return PerRound.Progress();
1336: 	}
1337: 	return ReloadDuration > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER ? FMath::Clamp(ReloadElapsed / ReloadDuration, 0.f, 1.f) : 1.f;
1338: }
1339: 
1340: // ---- Aim ----
1341: 
1342: void AGBGun::StartSecondary()
1343: {
1344: 	if (bEquipped)
1345: 	{
1346: 		SetAiming(true);
1347: 	}
1348: }
1349: 
1350: void AGBGun::StopSecondary()
1351: {
1352: 	SetAiming(false);
1353: }
1354: 
1355: void AGBGun::SetAiming(bool bInAiming)
1356: {
1357: 	if (bAiming == bInAiming)
1358: 	{
1359: 		return;
1360: 	}
1361: 	bAiming = bInAiming;
1362: 	if (AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner())
1363: 	{
1364: 		Player->SetMoveSpeedScale(bAiming ? GetStat(GBStat::AimMoveSpeedMult, 1.f) : 1.f);
1365: 	}
1366: }
1367: 
1368: void AGBGun::UpdateAimFOV(float DeltaSeconds, bool bInstant)
1369: {
1370: 	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
1371: 	UCameraComponent* Camera = Player ? Player->GetCamera() : nullptr;
1372: 	if (!Camera)
1373: 	{
1374: 		return;
1375: 	}
1376: 
1377: 	const float Target = bAiming ? 1.f : 0.f;
1378: 	if (AimAlpha == Target)
1379: 	{
1380: 		return;
1381: 	}
1382: 	if (AimAlpha <= 0.f)
1383: 	{
1384: 		HipFOV = Camera->FieldOfView;
1385: 	}
1386: 
1387: 	if (bInstant)
1388: 	{
1389: 		AimAlpha = Target;
1390: 	}
1391: 	else
1392: 	{
1393: 		const float AimTime = GetStat(GBWeaponStat::AimTime, 0.15f);
1394: 		const float Step = AimTime > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER ? DeltaSeconds / AimTime : 1.f;
1395: 		AimAlpha = FMath::Clamp(Target > AimAlpha ? AimAlpha + Step : AimAlpha - Step, 0.f, 1.f);
1396: 	}
1397: 	Camera->SetFieldOfView(FMath::Lerp(HipFOV, GetStat(GBStat::AimFOV, HipFOV), AimAlpha));
1398: }
1399: 
1400: void AGBGun::OnUnequipped()
1401: {
1402: 	CancelReload();
1403: 	bFireWhenLoaded = false;
1404: 	SetAiming(false);
1405: 	UpdateAimFOV(0.f, true);
1406: 	if (bViewModel)
1407: 	{
1408: 		KickElapsed = -1.f;
1409: 		ResetReloadVisuals();
1410: 		HideMuzzleFlash();
1411: 	}
1412: 	Super::OnUnequipped();
1413: }
1414: 
1415: void AGBGun::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
1416: {
1417: 	if (bReloading && ReloadsPerRound())
1418: 	{
1419: 		ReloadElapsed += DeltaSeconds;
1420: 		const int32 Moved = PerRound.Advance(DeltaSeconds, Ammo.InMagazine, Ammo.SpareMagazines, GetMagazineSize());
1421: 		if (Moved > 0)
1422: 		{
1423: 			NotifyEquipmentChanged();
1424: 			if (bFireWhenLoaded && Ammo.InMagazine > 0)
1425: 			{
1426: 				// Fire was pressed on an empty gun: the first shell is in, so the buffered shot may go (PerformAttack stops the reload).
1427: 				bFireWhenLoaded = false;
1428: 				bAttackQueued = true;
1429: 			}
1430: 		}
1431: 		if (!PerRound.bRunning)
1432: 		{
1433: 			FinishPerRoundReload();
1434: 		}
1435: 	}
1436: 	else if (bReloading)
1437: 	{
1438: 		ReloadElapsed += DeltaSeconds;
1439: 		if (ReloadElapsed >= ReloadDuration)
1440: 		{
1441: 			FinishReload();
1442: 		}
1443: 	}
1444: 	if (bEquipped)
1445: 	{
1446: 		UpdateAimFOV(DeltaSeconds, false);
1447: 	}
1448: 	if (bViewModel)
1449: 	{
1450: 		UpdateViewModel(DeltaSeconds);
1451: 	}
1452: 	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
1453: }
1454: 
1455: // ---- Shooting ----
1456: 
1457: bool AGBGun::PerformAttack()
1458: {
1459: 	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
1460: 	if (!Player || !GetWorld())
1461: 	{
1462: 		return false;
1463: 	}
1464: 	if (bReloading)
1465: 	{
1466: 		if (!ReloadsPerRound())
1467: 		{
1468: 			return false;
1469: 		}
1470: 		if (Ammo.InMagazine <= 0)
1471: 		{
1472: 			// Empty gun: the shot waits for the first shell to land (Tick), then the reload stops and the gun shoots.
1473: 			bFireWhenLoaded = true;
1474: 			return false;
1475: 		}
1476: 		CancelReload(); // fire stops a round-by-round reload; the shells already in stay in
1477: 	}
1478: 	if (Ammo.InMagazine <= 0)
1479: 	{
1480: 		Reload();
1481: 		return false;
1482: 	}
1483: 
1484: 	const FGBDamageInfo BaseInfo = MakeDamageInfo();
1485: 	const float SpreadDegrees = GetCurrentSpread();
1486: 
1487: 	--Ammo.InMagazine;
1488: 
1489: 	const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition();
1490: 	if (Def && Def->FireMode.Equals(TEXT("Projectile"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
1491: 	{
1492: 		FireProjectile(BaseInfo, SpreadDegrees);
1493: 	}
1494: 	else
1495: 	{
1496: 		FireHitscan(BaseInfo, SpreadDegrees);
1497: 	}
1498: 
1499: 	const float RecoilYaw = GetStat(GBStat::RecoilYaw, 0.f);
1500: 	Player->AddRecoil(GetStat(GBStat::RecoilPitch, 0.f), Random.FRandRange(-RecoilYaw, RecoilYaw), GetStat(GBStat::RecoilRecovery, 0.f));
1501: 
1502: 	if (bViewModel)
1503: 	{
1504: 		TriggerPullElapsed = 0.f;
1505: 		StartShotEffects();
1506: 		StartPump();
1507: 	}
1508: 	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
1509: 
1510: 	if (Ammo.InMagazine <= 0)
1511: 	{
1512: 		Reload();
1513: 	}
1514: 	return true;
1515: }
1516: 
1517: float AGBGun::GetCurrentSpread() const
1518: {
1519: 	const float Base = bAiming ? GetStat(GBStat::AimSpread, 0.f) : GetStat(GBStat::Spread, 0.f);
1520: 	const float Move = bAiming ? GetStat(GBStat::AimMoveSpread, 0.f) : GetStat(GBStat::MoveSpread, 0.f);
1521: 	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
1522: 	const UCharacterMovementComponent* MoveComp = Player ? Player->GetCharacterMovement() : nullptr;
1523: 	if (!MoveComp)
1524: 	{
1525: 		return FMath::Max(0.f, Base);
1526: 	}
1527: 	// Full bonus at the current max walk speed (already lowered while aiming), so slow aimed steps still count.
1528: 	return UGBCombatLibrary::ComputeMovementSpread(Base, Move, MoveComp->Velocity.Size2D(), MoveComp->MaxWalkSpeed);
1529: }

1531: void AGBGun::FireHitscan(const FGBDamageInfo& BaseInfo, float SpreadDegrees)
1532: {
1533: 	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
1534: 	UWorld* World = GetWorld();

#include "Weapons/GBHeldItem.h"

#include "Combat/GBCombatLibrary.h"
#include "Combat/GBStatusEffectComponent.h"
#include "Core/GBStatNames.h"
#include "Data/GBDataSubsystem.h"

#include "Spells/GBSpellbookComponent.h"

AGBHeldItem::AGBHeldItem()
{
	PrimaryActorTick.bCanEverTick = true;
	PrimaryActorTick.bStartWithTickEnabled = true;

	Root = CreateDefaultSubobject<USceneComponent>(TEXT("Root"));
	RootComponent = Root;

	SetActorEnableCollision(false);
}

void AGBHeldItem::InitFromDefinition(const FGBWeaponDefinition& Definition, AGBPlayerCharacter* InOwner)
{
	WeaponId = Definition.Id;
	Slot = Definition.Slot;
	Element = Definition.Element;
	PlayerOwner = InOwner;
	SetOwner(InOwner);
	SetInstigator(InOwner);
	Random.GenerateNewSeed();
}

const FGBWeaponDefinition* AGBHeldItem::GetDefinition() const
{
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	return Data ? Data->FindWeapon(WeaponId) : nullptr;
}

FString AGBHeldItem::GetDisplayName() const
{
	const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition();
	return (Def && !Def->DisplayName.IsEmpty()) ? Def->DisplayName : WeaponId.ToString();
}

float AGBHeldItem::GetStat(FName Stat, float DefaultValue) const
{
	float Base = DefaultValue;
	if (const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition())
	{
		if (const float* Value = Def->Stats.Find(Stat))
		{
			Base = *Value;
		}
	}

	if (const UGBStatsComponent* Stats = GetOwnerStats())
	{
		TArray<FName, TInlineAllocator<3>> Keys;
		Keys.Add(GBStat::WeaponKey(WeaponId, Stat));
		Keys.Add(GBStat::WeaponSlotKey(Slot, Stat));
		Keys.Add(GBStat::WeaponAllKey(Stat));
		return Stats->EvaluateKeys(Keys, Base);
	}
	return Base;
}

void AGBHeldItem::OnEquipped()
{
	bEquipped = true;
	SetActorHiddenInGame(false);
}

void AGBHeldItem::OnUnequipped()
{
	bEquipped = false;
	bTriggerHeld = false;
	bAttackQueued = false;
	SetActorHiddenInGame(true);
}

EGBHudSkin AGBHeldItem::GetHudSkin() const
{
	return Slot == EGBHeldSlot::Gun ? EGBHudSkin::Cyberpunk : EGBHudSkin::Rpg90s;
}

float AGBHeldItem::GetTimeSinceLastAttack() const
{
	const UWorld* World = GetWorld();
	return World ? static_cast<float>(World->GetTimeSeconds() - LastAttackTime) : 1000.f;
}

bool AGBHeldItem::IsAutomatic() const
{
	const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition();
	return Def && Def->bAutomatic;
}

UGBStatsComponent* AGBHeldItem::GetOwnerStats() const
{
	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	return Player ? Player->GetStats() : nullptr;
}

bool AGBHeldItem::GetActiveEnchant(EGBElement& OutElement, FName& OutOnHitEffect) const
{
	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	const UGBStatusEffectComponent* Effects = Player ? Player->GetStatusEffects() : nullptr;
	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
	if (!Effects || !Data)
	{
		return false;
	}

	for (const FGBActiveStatusEffect& Active : Effects->GetActiveEffects())
	{
		const FGBStatusEffectDefinition* Def = Data->FindStatusEffect(Active.EffectId);
		if (!Def)
		{
			continue;
		}
		const FString* ElementName = Def->Strings.Find(GBWeaponString::WeaponElement);
		if (!ElementName || ElementName->IsEmpty())
		{
			continue;
		}
		const int64 Value = StaticEnum<EGBElement>()->GetValueByNameString(*ElementName);
		if (Value == INDEX_NONE)
		{
			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Status effect '%s': unknown WeaponElement '%s'"), *Active.EffectId.ToString(), **ElementName);
			continue;
		}
		OutElement = static_cast<EGBElement>(Value);
		const FString* Effect = Def->Strings.Find(GBWeaponString::WeaponOnHitEffect);
		OutOnHitEffect = (Effect && !Effect->IsEmpty()) ? FName(**Effect) : NAME_None;
		return true;
	}
	return false;
}

FGBDamageInfo AGBHeldItem::MakeDamageInfo() const
{
	FGBDamageInfo Info;
	Info.Amount = GetStat(GBStat::Damage);
	Info.Element = Element;
	Info.Instigator = GetPlayerOwner();
	Info.Causer = const_cast<AGBHeldItem*>(this);
	Info.SourceId = WeaponId;

	if (const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition())
	{
		if (const FString* Effect = Def->Strings.Find(GBString::OnHitEffect))
		{
			if (!Effect->IsEmpty())
			{
				Info.OnHitEffect = FName(**Effect);
			}
		}
	}

	// The enchant's damage multiplier arrives by itself through its "Weapon.Gun.Damage" modifier.
	bool bElementFromEnchant = false;
	EGBElement EnchantElement = EGBElement::Physical;
	FName EnchantEffect = NAME_None;
	if (AcceptsEnchant() && GetActiveEnchant(EnchantElement, EnchantEffect))
	{
		Info.Element = EnchantElement;
		if (!EnchantEffect.IsNone())
		{
			Info.OnHitEffect = EnchantEffect;
		}
		bElementFromEnchant = true;
	}

	const int32 ElementIndex = GBElemental::ElementIndex(Info.Element);
	if (ElementIndex >= 0)
	{
		Info.BuildUp = GetStat(GBElemental::WeaponBuildUpKey(ElementIndex), 0.f);
		if (bElementFromEnchant)
		{
			Info.BuildUp *= GetStat(GBStat::EnchantBuildUpMult, 1.f);
		}
		const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
		const float DefaultMult = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBStat::WeakPointBuildUpMult, 1.f) : 1.f;
		Info.WeakPointBuildUpMult = GetStat(GBStat::WeakPointBuildUpMult, DefaultMult);
	}
	Info.BuildUpEventId = UGBCombatLibrary::NewBuildUpEventId();
	return Info;
}

void AGBHeldItem::PressTrigger()
{
	bTriggerHeld = true;
	bAttackQueued = true;
	UpdateTrigger();
}

void AGBHeldItem::ReleaseTrigger()
{
	bTriggerHeld = false;
	bAttackQueued = false;
}

bool AGBHeldItem::IsAttackReady() const
{
	return GetTimeSinceLastAttack() >= GetStat(GBStat::FireInterval, 0.f);
}

void AGBHeldItem::UpdateTrigger()
{
	if (!bEquipped || !(bAttackQueued || (bTriggerHeld && IsAutomatic())))
	{
		return;
	}
	if (!IsAttackReady())
	{
		return; // a pressed shot stays queued until the fire interval has passed
	}

	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	const UGBSpellbookComponent* Spellbook = Player ? Player->GetSpellbook() : nullptr;
	if (!Player || Player->IsDead() || (Spellbook && Spellbook->IsBusy()))
	{
		bAttackQueued = false;
		return;
	}

	if (PerformAttack())
	{
		if (const UWorld* World = GetWorld())
		{
			LastAttackTime = World->GetTimeSeconds();
		}
	}
	bAttackQueued = false;
}

void AGBHeldItem::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
{
	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
	UpdateTrigger();
}

FVector AGBHeldItem::FindAimPoint(float Range) const
{
	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	if (!Player)
	{
		return GetActorLocation();
	}
	const FVector Start = Player->GetAimOrigin();
	const FVector End = Start + Player->GetAimDirection() * Range;

	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBAimPoint), false, Player);
	Params.AddIgnoredActor(this);
	FHitResult Hit;
	if (GetWorld() && GetWorld()->LineTraceSingleByChannel(Hit, Start, End, GBWeapon::AimTraceChannel, Params))
	{
		return Hit.ImpactPoint;
	}
	return End;
}

void AGBHeldItem::NotifyEquipmentChanged() const
{
	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
	if (UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = Player ? Player->GetEquipment() : nullptr)
	{
		Equipment->OnEquipmentChanged.Broadcast();
	}
}

+
+// ---- Round-by-round reload (the shotgun): pure stepping, FGBPerRoundReload ----
+
+IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBWeaponsPerRoundFullTest, "Glyphbound.Weapons.PerRoundFullReload",
+	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
+bool FGBWeaponsPerRoundFullTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
+{
+	// Empty 6-round gun, 24 in reserve, ReloadTime 2 s: six rounds, done at 2 s and not a cycle earlier or later.
+	int32 Rounds = 0;
+	int32 Reserve = 24;
+	FGBPerRoundReload Reload;
+	Reload.Begin(Rounds, Reserve, 6, 2.f);
+	TestTrue(TEXT("A reload with space and reserve runs"), Reload.bRunning);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("It plans to load the whole magazine"), Reload.Planned, 6);
+
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Just under one cycle moves nothing"), Reload.Advance(2.f / 6.f - 0.01f, Rounds, Reserve, 6), 0);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Nothing is loaded before a cycle ends"), Rounds, 0);
+
+	Reload.Advance(0.02f, Rounds, Reserve, 6);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("The first round lands when its cycle ends"), Rounds, 1);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("It came out of the reserve"), Reserve, 23);
+
+	// Up to just before the end of the reload: five rounds, still running.
+	Reload.Advance(2.f - 2.f / 6.f - 0.02f, Rounds, Reserve, 6);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Five rounds a hair before ReloadTime"), Rounds, 5);
+	TestTrue(TEXT("Still running a hair before ReloadTime"), Reload.bRunning);
+
+	Reload.Advance(0.04f, Rounds, Reserve, 6);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Six rounds at ReloadTime"), Rounds, 6);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Exactly the magazine's worth left the reserve"), Reserve, 18);
+	TestFalse(TEXT("A full gun ends the reload"), Reload.bRunning);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Progress is complete"), Reload.Progress(), 1.f);
+	return true;
+}
+
+IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBWeaponsPerRoundReserveTest, "Glyphbound.Weapons.PerRoundShortReserve",
+	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
+bool FGBWeaponsPerRoundReserveTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
+{
+	// 2 rounds in a 6-round gun but only 1 in reserve: it loads that one, stops, and nothing goes negative.
+	int32 Rounds = 2;
+	int32 Reserve = 1;
+	FGBPerRoundReload Reload;
+	Reload.Begin(Rounds, Reserve, 6, 2.f);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("It plans only what the reserve can give"), Reload.Planned, 1);
+	Reload.Advance(10.f, Rounds, Reserve, 6);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("The one round is loaded"), Rounds, 3);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("The reserve is empty, not negative"), Reserve, 0);
+	TestFalse(TEXT("It stops when the reserve is gone"), Reload.bRunning);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Further time moves nothing"), Reload.Advance(10.f, Rounds, Reserve, 6), 0);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Still 3 rounds"), Rounds, 3);
+
+	// Nothing to load at all: it never runs.
+	int32 FullRounds = 6;
+	int32 FullReserve = 24;
+	FGBPerRoundReload Full;
+	Full.Begin(FullRounds, FullReserve, 6, 2.f);
+	TestFalse(TEXT("A full gun has nothing to load"), Full.bRunning);
+	int32 NoRounds = 0;
+	int32 NoReserve = 0;
+	FGBPerRoundReload Dry;
+	Dry.Begin(NoRounds, NoReserve, 6, 2.f);
+	TestFalse(TEXT("An empty reserve has nothing to load"), Dry.bRunning);
+	return true;
+}
+
+IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBWeaponsPerRoundBigStepTest, "Glyphbound.Weapons.PerRoundLongFrame",
+	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
+bool FGBWeaponsPerRoundBigStepTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
+{
+	// One frame longer than three cycles (3.4 cycles of 1/3 s): three rounds move and the 0.4 cycle is kept.
+	const float Cycle = 2.f / 6.f;
+	int32 Rounds = 0;
+	int32 Reserve = 24;
+	FGBPerRoundReload Reload;
+	Reload.Begin(Rounds, Reserve, 6, 2.f);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Three rounds in one long frame"), Reload.Advance(3.4f * Cycle, Rounds, Reserve, 6), 3);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Gun"), Rounds, 3);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Reserve"), Reserve, 21);
+	TestTrue(TEXT("The remainder of the time is kept"), FMath::IsNearlyEqual(Reload.CycleElapsed, 0.4f * Cycle, 0.001f));
+
+	// The kept 0.4 cycle plus 0.7 more finishes the fourth round (and starts the next one).
+	TestEqual(TEXT("The kept time counts towards the next round"), Reload.Advance(0.7f * Cycle, Rounds, Reserve, 6), 1);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Four rounds"), Rounds, 4);
+	return true;
+}
+
+IMPLEMENT_SIMPLE_AUTOMATION_TEST(FGBWeaponsPerRoundStopTest, "Glyphbound.Weapons.PerRoundStopPartWay",
+	EAutomationTestFlags::EditorContext | EAutomationTestFlags::EngineFilter)
+bool FGBWeaponsPerRoundStopTest::RunTest(const FString& Parameters)
+{
+	// Two and a half cycles in, the reload is stopped: two rounds stay in the gun, the half-loaded one is not lost.
+	const float Cycle = 2.f / 6.f;
+	int32 Rounds = 1;
+	int32 Reserve = 24;
+	FGBPerRoundReload Reload;
+	Reload.Begin(Rounds, Reserve, 6, 2.f);
+	Reload.Advance(2.5f * Cycle, Rounds, Reserve, 6);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Two rounds moved so far"), Rounds, 3);
+	Reload.Stop();
+	TestFalse(TEXT("Stopped"), Reload.bRunning);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("The rounds already moved stay in the gun"), Rounds, 3);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Only the moved rounds left the reserve; the one in flight is still there"), Reserve, 22);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Rounds in the gun and in the reserve still add up"), Rounds + Reserve, 25);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("A stopped reload moves nothing more"), Reload.Advance(5.f, Rounds, Reserve, 6), 0);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Gun unchanged after the stop"), Rounds, 3);
+	TestEqual(TEXT("Reserve unchanged after the stop"), Reserve, 22);
+	return true;
+}

10:- v0.33 (2026-11-12): owner, on the shotgun: it loads **shell by shell** (not a magazine swap), and its **forend pumps** after every shot. Its model is a Tripo science-fiction shotgun. Rules and the architect's provisional numbers in §18 "Reload".
18:- v0.24 (2026-11-12): owner: the weapon tree is reorganized (Heavy Rounds on top; a handling branch and a precision branch; three weapon specializations on one row) and gets four new perks: Rapid Reload, Precision Recovery, Arcane Conduit, Deadeye. See §18 "Weapon tree".
35:- v0.7 (2026-11-12): full story (the entity, the false voice, the Valkyrie); elemental zones in the castle unlock the rest of each element; air passives learned the same way; shotgun and rifle dropped by enemies; era travel restores everything (bonfire-like); remaining keys confirmed.
51:- **Tuning data (decided by the architect):** every gameplay number lives in text files, `Glyphbound/Content/Data/*.json` (spells, weapons, enemies, items, skills, player, QTE...). They ship as loose files: the owner can change numbers in a packaged build with a text editor, and `gb.ReloadData` in the console reloads them while playing. This replaces the data tables suggested earlier (git only carries text in this team, and AI agents edit text reliably).
139:  - **Weapon tree:** damage, fire rate, **recoil** (better aim), **damage by distance** (shotgun stronger up close, rifle stronger far away), **headshot damage**, reload speed, healing on precise kills, enchant build-up. Layout in §18 "Weapon tree".
193:- **Per source:** each spell has its own build-up per hit or per tick (`BuildUp` in `Spells.json`); each weapon has its own build-up per element (`FireBuildUp`, `LightningBuildUp`, `IceBuildUp` in `Weapons.json`). An unenchanted gun builds up nothing; the apprentice staff (arcane) builds up nothing. One shot counts once per enemy: the eight pellets of a shotgun shot give one build-up to each enemy they hit.
254:- Pistol, shotgun, assault rifle.
255:- The **first gun** is dropped by one of the first armed orcs in the future (§4). The **shotgun and the rifle** are dropped later by enemies, picked up from the floor.
259:- **Gun models (v0.25):** the pistol is the first real 3D model (made with Tripo on ASSETS, finished in Blender on CENTRAL); the shotgun and rifle keep placeholder shapes until their models exist. The pistol's trigger moves on each shot and its magazine slides out and back in during the reload. **Glow (provisional, architect):** the gun's glowing parts show its own green when not enchanted; while the enchant runs they take the element's colour, and the energy bar on the side shows the enchant time left (full when cast, empty when it ends). **Owner, v0.30:** the bar's 13 cells show the time left rounded up, so the last cell goes dark exactly when the enchant ends (before, it went dark half a cell early); the weak-point light is half as long as before: full for 0.06 s, then a 0.15 s fade (`PistolWeakPointFlashHold` / `PistolWeakPointFlashFade` in `Game.json`).
299:| Reload (gun) | R | Standard shooter layout |
455:- **Reload:** the bullets left in the old magazine are lost (classic tactical style). A gun reloads by itself when its magazine runs dry. Ammo pickups give one spare magazine, up to the gun's maximum.
456:  - **The shotgun loads shell by shell (owner, 2026-11-12).** One shell goes in at a time; nothing is lost by reloading early, and the reload can stop part-way. Provisional (architect): its reserve is counted in shells (4 magazines x 6 = 24, the same total as before); each shell takes `ReloadTime / MagazineSize` (0.33 s), so a full reload from empty still takes the 2 s the balance tables use and Rapid Reload speeds it the same way; pressing fire during the reload stops it and shoots as soon as a shell is in (the shell on its way in is not lost: it stays in the reserve); an ammo pickup gives one magazine's worth of shells (6); an empty shotgun still starts reloading by itself.
457:  - **The shotgun's forend pumps after every shot (owner, 2026-11-12).** Provisional (architect): it is only the look; the time between shots stays `FireInterval` (0.8 s) and the pump plays inside it.
460:- **Spread while moving (owner, 2026-11-12; numbers by the architect, provisional):** each gun adds `MoveSpread` (hip fire) or `AimMoveSpread` (aiming) degrees to its spread, scaled by the player's horizontal speed over the current walk speed (0 standing still, full bonus at full walk speed or faster; aiming already lowers the walk speed, so a slow aimed walk counts as full). Pistol 1.2 -> 3.7 hip, 0.25 -> 1.25 aimed; shotgun 6 -> 9, 4.5 -> 6.5; rifle 1.8 -> 4.8, 0.35 -> 1.55 (`Weapons.json`, modifiable by perks as `Weapon.<Id>.MoveSpread`). Jumping counts by its horizontal speed only.
462:- **Weapon tree (owner, v0.24):** Heavy Rounds (+10% gun damage) on top. **Handling** branch (left): Quick Trigger (-8% time between shots) -> Rapid Reload (-10% reload time per rank, -30% at 3) and Steady Grip (-20% recoil per rank, now pitch and yaw). **Precision** branch (right): Marksman (+0.25x head / weak point damage) -> Precision Recovery and Arcane Conduit (+10% elemental build-up from enchanted weapons per rank, +30% at 3; the sword's own element does not count). **Specializations** (bottom row, all needing only Heavy Rounds, so no gun is favored): Deadeye (pistol, +0.25x head / weak point damage per rank: 2.0 -> 2.25 / 2.5 / 2.75), Point Blank (shotgun, +0.2x up close), Long Shot (rifle, +0.2x far; owner, v0.29, was +0.15x). Every perk: 3 ranks, 1 point per rank (Marksman 2, Precision Recovery 2 since v0.27). **Precision Recovery:** killing an enemy with a head / weak point hit heals a fixed **3% of max health**, with an internal cooldown of **6 / 5 / 4 s** by rank; once per enemy death (a shotgun's pellets never heal more than once), never for a head hit that does not kill (in a horde game that would be near-unlimited healing). Provisional (architect): the prerequisites inside each branch (the owner's sketch only shows the arrows Heavy Rounds -> both branches, Quick Trigger -> Rapid Reload, Marksman -> Precision Recovery; Steady Grip hangs from Quick Trigger and Arcane Conduit from Marksman); only the killing blow counts, from any weapon, spells excluded (they never flag weak points).
488:- **Head / weak point bonus per weapon** (owner, 2026-11-12): pistol x2; shotgun, rifle, staffs and sword x1.5. **Perks can raise it**: the weapon tree node "Marksman" adds +0.25 per rank (3 ranks) to every weapon, staff and sword included (provisional numbers). Spell projectiles get no head bonus unless a spell defines one.
493:- **Guns (v0.28):** pistol 20 damage every 0.25 s, 15 rounds, 6 spare magazines; shotgun 8 pellets of 10 every 0.8 s, 6 rounds, 4 spare, strong up close; rifle automatic, 12 damage every 0.1 s, 30 rounds, 5 spare, stronger far away. Headshots: pistol x2, shotgun and rifle x1.5. Elemental sword 50 every 0.5 s. Why: §19.
497:- **Build-up per source (v0.28):** fireball 40 (25-55 with QTE power), fire wall 15 per 0.25 s tick, flamethrower 8 per 0.3 s tick, lightning bolt 35 (20-50), lightning rune 60 (35-85), static orb 12 per tick, ice floor 5 per 0.5 s tick (60 at most over its 6 s, so it never fills the bar alone), electric grenade 60. Weapons per hit: pistol 15, shotgun 40 per shot, rifle 6, elemental sword 25, ember staff 18 (fire): every weapon fills about 50-60 per second of fire. Weak point bonus x1.5.
505:**Every number here is provisional and lives in `Glyphbound/Content/Data/*.json`.** Nothing needs a recompile: edit the file and run `gb.ReloadData` in the console (it also works in the packaged game). `node Tools/Balance/balance_model.js` prints every table below from the data, so after changing a number the tables can be checked again in a second. The numbers were reviewed by a second model (GPT-6-Astra, medium effort) before the first build; what it found is at the end.
590:Both trees cost 78 points (weapons 36, spells 42). **Budget: 36 points before the Entity (46%).** Example: Heavy Rounds, Quick Trigger, Rapid Reload, Marksman and Deadeye at rank 3 (18 points, a finished pistol), Point Blank 3 (the shotgun half-way), Frugal Casting 3 (6 points) and three spell upgrades at rank 3 (9 points): a build, not the whole tree.
611:Enemy AI fractions, projectile lifetime and knockback lift, grenade blast falloff and bounce, the fire wall's touch margin, the ice shield's block arc, player acceleration, friction and look speed, what a respawn and an era travel restore, and what a failed QTE costs. All keep their old value; the list is in the day entry. A spell already in the world keeps the numbers it was cast with: cast it again after `gb.ReloadData`.

  52: 		if (const float* Value = Def->Stats.Find(Stat))
  53: 		{
  54: 			Base = *Value;
  55: 		}
  56: 	}
  57: 
  58: 	if (const UGBStatsComponent* Stats = GetOwnerStats())
  59: 	{
  60: 		TArray<FName, TInlineAllocator<3>> Keys;
  61: 		Keys.Add(GBStat::WeaponKey(WeaponId, Stat));
  62: 		Keys.Add(GBStat::WeaponSlotKey(Slot, Stat));
  63: 		Keys.Add(GBStat::WeaponAllKey(Stat));
  64: 		return Stats->EvaluateKeys(Keys, Base);
  65: 	}
  66: 	return Base;
  67: }
  68: 
  69: void AGBHeldItem::OnEquipped()
  70: {
  71: 	bEquipped = true;
  72: 	SetActorHiddenInGame(false);
  73: }

  76: {
  77: 	bEquipped = false;
  78: 	bTriggerHeld = false;
  79: 	bAttackQueued = false;
  80: 	SetActorHiddenInGame(true);
  81: }

 105: 
 106: bool AGBHeldItem::GetActiveEnchant(EGBElement& OutElement, FName& OutOnHitEffect) const
 107: {
 108: 	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
 109: 	const UGBStatusEffectComponent* Effects = Player ? Player->GetStatusEffects() : nullptr;
 110: 	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
 111: 	if (!Effects || !Data)
 112: 	{
 113: 		return false;
 114: 	}
 115: 
 116: 	for (const FGBActiveStatusEffect& Active : Effects->GetActiveEffects())
 117: 	{
 118: 		const FGBStatusEffectDefinition* Def = Data->FindStatusEffect(Active.EffectId);
 119: 		if (!Def)
 120: 		{
 121: 			continue;
 122: 		}
 123: 		const FString* ElementName = Def->Strings.Find(GBWeaponString::WeaponElement);
 124: 		if (!ElementName || ElementName->IsEmpty())
 125: 		{
 126: 			continue;
 127: 		}
 128: 		const int64 Value = StaticEnum<EGBElement>()->GetValueByNameString(*ElementName);
 129: 		if (Value == INDEX_NONE)
 130: 		{
 131: 			UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("Status effect '%s': unknown WeaponElement '%s'"), *Active.EffectId.ToString(), **ElementName);
 132: 			continue;
 133: 		}
 134: 		OutElement = static_cast<EGBElement>(Value);
 135: 		const FString* Effect = Def->Strings.Find(GBWeaponString::WeaponOnHitEffect);
 136: 		OutOnHitEffect = (Effect && !Effect->IsEmpty()) ? FName(**Effect) : NAME_None;
 137: 		return true;
 138: 	}
 139: 	return false;
 140: }
 141: 
 142: FGBDamageInfo AGBHeldItem::MakeDamageInfo() const
 143: {
 144: 	FGBDamageInfo Info;
 145: 	Info.Amount = GetStat(GBStat::Damage);
 146: 	Info.Element = Element;
 147: 	Info.Instigator = GetPlayerOwner();
 148: 	Info.Causer = const_cast<AGBHeldItem*>(this);
 149: 	Info.SourceId = WeaponId;
 150: 
 151: 	if (const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition())
 152: 	{
 153: 		if (const FString* Effect = Def->Strings.Find(GBString::OnHitEffect))
 154: 		{
 155: 			if (!Effect->IsEmpty())
 156: 			{
 157: 				Info.OnHitEffect = FName(**Effect);
 158: 			}
 159: 		}
 160: 	}
 161: 
 162: 	// The enchant's damage multiplier arrives by itself through its "Weapon.Gun.Damage" modifier.
 163: 	bool bElementFromEnchant = false;
 164: 	EGBElement EnchantElement = EGBElement::Physical;
 165: 	FName EnchantEffect = NAME_None;
 166: 	if (AcceptsEnchant() && GetActiveEnchant(EnchantElement, EnchantEffect))
 167: 	{
 168: 		Info.Element = EnchantElement;
 169: 		if (!EnchantEffect.IsNone())
 170: 		{
 171: 			Info.OnHitEffect = EnchantEffect;
 172: 		}
 173: 		bElementFromEnchant = true;
 174: 	}
 175: 
 176: 	const int32 ElementIndex = GBElemental::ElementIndex(Info.Element);
 177: 	if (ElementIndex >= 0)
 178: 	{
 179: 		Info.BuildUp = GetStat(GBElemental::WeaponBuildUpKey(ElementIndex), 0.f);
 180: 		if (bElementFromEnchant)
 181: 		{
 182: 			Info.BuildUp *= GetStat(GBStat::EnchantBuildUpMult, 1.f);
 183: 		}
 184: 		const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
 185: 		const float DefaultMult = Data ? Data->GetGameValue(GBStat::WeakPointBuildUpMult, 1.f) : 1.f;
 186: 		Info.WeakPointBuildUpMult = GetStat(GBStat::WeakPointBuildUpMult, DefaultMult);
 187: 	}
 188: 	Info.BuildUpEventId = UGBCombatLibrary::NewBuildUpEventId();
 189: 	return Info;
 190: }
 191: 
 192: void AGBHeldItem::PressTrigger()
 193: {
 194: 	bTriggerHeld = true;
 195: 	bAttackQueued = true;
 196: 	UpdateTrigger();
 197: }
 198: 
 199: void AGBHeldItem::ReleaseTrigger()
 200: {
 201: 	bTriggerHeld = false;
 202: 	bAttackQueued = false;
 203: }
 204: 
 205: bool AGBHeldItem::IsAttackReady() const
 206: {
 207: 	return GetTimeSinceLastAttack() >= GetStat(GBStat::FireInterval, 0.f);
 208: }
 209: 
 210: void AGBHeldItem::UpdateTrigger()
 211: {
 212: 	if (!bEquipped || !(bAttackQueued || (bTriggerHeld && IsAutomatic())))
 213: 	{
 214: 		return;
 215: 	}
 216: 	if (!IsAttackReady())
 217: 	{
 218: 		return; // a pressed shot stays queued until the fire interval has passed
 219: 	}
 220: 
 221: 	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
 222: 	const UGBSpellbookComponent* Spellbook = Player ? Player->GetSpellbook() : nullptr;
 223: 	if (!Player || Player->IsDead() || (Spellbook && Spellbook->IsBusy()))
 224: 	{
 225: 		bAttackQueued = false;
 226: 		return;
 227: 	}
 228: 
 229: 	if (PerformAttack())
 230: 	{
 231: 		if (const UWorld* World = GetWorld())
 232: 		{
 233: 			LastAttackTime = World->GetTimeSeconds();
 234: 		}
 235: 	}
 236: 	bAttackQueued = false;
 237: }
 238: 
 239: void AGBHeldItem::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
 240: {
 241: 	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
 242: 	UpdateTrigger();
 243: }
 244: 
 245: FVector AGBHeldItem::FindAimPoint(float Range) const
 246: {
 247: 	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
 248: 	if (!Player)
 249: 	{
 250: 		return GetActorLocation();
 251: 	}
 252: 	const FVector Start = Player->GetAimOrigin();
 253: 	const FVector End = Start + Player->GetAimDirection() * Range;
 254: 
 255: 	FCollisionQueryParams Params(SCENE_QUERY_STAT(GBAimPoint), false, Player);
 256: 	Params.AddIgnoredActor(this);
 257: 	FHitResult Hit;
 258: 	if (GetWorld() && GetWorld()->LineTraceSingleByChannel(Hit, Start, End, GBWeapon::AimTraceChannel, Params))
 259: 	{
 260: 		return Hit.ImpactPoint;
 261: 	}
 262: 	return End;
 263: }
 264: 
 265: void AGBHeldItem::NotifyEquipmentChanged() const
 266: {
 267: 	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
 268: 	if (UGBEquipmentComponent* Equipment = Player ? Player->GetEquipment() : nullptr)
 269: 	{
 270: 		Equipment->OnEquipmentChanged.Broadcast();
 271: 	}
 272: }
1415: void AGBGun::Tick(float DeltaSeconds)
1416: {
1417: 	if (bReloading && ReloadsPerRound())
1418: 	{
1419: 		ReloadElapsed += DeltaSeconds;
1420: 		const int32 Moved = PerRound.Advance(DeltaSeconds, Ammo.InMagazine, Ammo.SpareMagazines, GetMagazineSize());
1421: 		if (Moved > 0)
1422: 		{
1423: 			NotifyEquipmentChanged();
1424: 			if (bFireWhenLoaded && Ammo.InMagazine > 0)
1425: 			{
1426: 				// Fire was pressed on an empty gun: the first shell is in, so the buffered shot may go (PerformAttack stops the reload).
1427: 				bFireWhenLoaded = false;
1428: 				bAttackQueued = true;
1429: 			}
1430: 		}
1431: 		if (!PerRound.bRunning)
1432: 		{
1433: 			FinishPerRoundReload();
1434: 		}
1435: 	}
1436: 	else if (bReloading)
1437: 	{
1438: 		ReloadElapsed += DeltaSeconds;
1439: 		if (ReloadElapsed >= ReloadDuration)
1440: 		{
1441: 			FinishReload();
1442: 		}
1443: 	}
1444: 	if (bEquipped)
1445: 	{
1446: 		UpdateAimFOV(DeltaSeconds, false);
1447: 	}
1448: 	if (bViewModel)
1449: 	{
1450: 		UpdateViewModel(DeltaSeconds);
1451: 	}
1452: 	Super::Tick(DeltaSeconds);
1453: }
1454: 
1455: // ---- Shooting ----
1456: 
1457: bool AGBGun::PerformAttack()
1458: {
1459: 	AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
1460: 	if (!Player || !GetWorld())
1461: 	{
1462: 		return false;
1463: 	}
1464: 	if (bReloading)
1465: 	{
1466: 		if (!ReloadsPerRound())
1467: 		{
1468: 			return false;
1469: 		}
1470: 		if (Ammo.InMagazine <= 0)
1471: 		{
1472: 			// Empty gun: the shot waits for the first shell to land (Tick), then the reload stops and the gun shoots.
1473: 			bFireWhenLoaded = true;
1474: 			return false;
1475: 		}
1476: 		CancelReload(); // fire stops a round-by-round reload; the shells already in stay in
1477: 	}
1478: 	if (Ammo.InMagazine <= 0)
1479: 	{
1480: 		Reload();
1481: 		return false;
1482: 	}
1483: 
1484: 	const FGBDamageInfo BaseInfo = MakeDamageInfo();
1485: 	const float SpreadDegrees = GetCurrentSpread();
1486: 
1487: 	--Ammo.InMagazine;
1488: 
1489: 	const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = GetDefinition();
1490: 	if (Def && Def->FireMode.Equals(TEXT("Projectile"), ESearchCase::IgnoreCase))
1491: 	{
1492: 		FireProjectile(BaseInfo, SpreadDegrees);
1493: 	}
1494: 	else
1495: 	{
1496: 		FireHitscan(BaseInfo, SpreadDegrees);
1497: 	}
1498: 
1499: 	const float RecoilYaw = GetStat(GBStat::RecoilYaw, 0.f);
1500: 	Player->AddRecoil(GetStat(GBStat::RecoilPitch, 0.f), Random.FRandRange(-RecoilYaw, RecoilYaw), GetStat(GBStat::RecoilRecovery, 0.f));
1501: 
1502: 	if (bViewModel)
1503: 	{
1504: 		TriggerPullElapsed = 0.f;
1505: 		StartShotEffects();
1506: 		StartPump();
1507: 	}
1508: 	NotifyEquipmentChanged();
1509: 
1510: 	if (Ammo.InMagazine <= 0)
1511: 	{
1512: 		Reload();
1513: 	}
1514: 	return true;
1515: }
1516: 
1517: float AGBGun::GetCurrentSpread() const
1518: {
1519: 	const float Base = bAiming ? GetStat(GBStat::AimSpread, 0.f) : GetStat(GBStat::Spread, 0.f);
1520: 	const float Move = bAiming ? GetStat(GBStat::AimMoveSpread, 0.f) : GetStat(GBStat::MoveSpread, 0.f);
1521: 	const AGBPlayerCharacter* Player = GetPlayerOwner();
1522: 	const UCharacterMovementComponent* MoveComp = Player ? Player->GetCharacterMovement() : nullptr;
1523: 	if (!MoveComp)
1524: 	{
1525: 		return FMath::Max(0.f, Base);
1526: 	}
1527: 	// Full bonus at the current max walk speed (already lowered while aiming), so slow aimed steps still count.
1528: 	return UGBCombatLibrary::ComputeMovementSpread(Base, Move, MoveComp->Velocity.Size2D(), MoveComp->MaxWalkSpeed);
1529: }

  82: bool UGBEquipmentComponent::OwnsWeapon(FName WeaponId) const
  83: {
  84: 	return !WeaponId.IsNone() && OwnedWeapons.Contains(WeaponId);
  85: }

  87: bool UGBEquipmentComponent::IsWeaponEquipped(FName WeaponId) const
  88: {
  89: 	return FindEquippedItem(WeaponId) != nullptr;
  90: }
  91: 
  92: FGBAmmoState UGBEquipmentComponent::ComputeFullAmmo(FName WeaponId) const
  93: {
  94: 	FGBAmmoState Full;
  95: 	const UGBDataSubsystem* Data = UGBDataSubsystem::Get(this);
  96: 	const FGBWeaponDefinition* Def = Data ? Data->FindWeapon(WeaponId) : nullptr;
  97: 	if (!Def || Def->Slot != EGBHeldSlot::Gun)
  98: 	{
  99: 		return Full;
 100: 	}
 101: 	const UGBStatsComponent* Stats = UGBStatsComponent::Find(GetOwner());
 102: 	// Same keys and defaults as AGBHeldItem::GetStat / AGBGun, so a stored gun refills to what it would hold in hand.
 103: 	auto ReadStat = [Def, Stats, WeaponId](FName Stat, float DefaultValue)
 104: 	{
 105: 		const float* Found = Def->Stats.Find(Stat);
 106: 		const float Base = Found ? *Found : DefaultValue;
 107: 		if (!Stats)
 108: 		{
 109: 			return Base;
 110: 		}
 111: 		TArray<FName, TInlineAllocator<3>> Keys;
 112: 		Keys.Add(GBStat::WeaponKey(WeaponId, Stat));
 113: 		Keys.Add(GBStat::WeaponSlotKey(EGBHeldSlot::Gun, Stat));
 114: 		Keys.Add(GBStat::WeaponAllKey(Stat));
 115: 		return Stats->EvaluateKeys(Keys, Base);
 116: 	};
 117: 	Full.InMagazine = FMath::Max(0, FMath::RoundToInt(ReadStat(GBStat::MagazineSize, 1.f)));
 118: 	Full.SpareMagazines = FMath::Max(0, FMath::RoundToInt(ReadStat(GBStat::SpareMagazines, 0.f)));
 119: 	if (ReadStat(GBStat::ReloadPerRound, 0.f) > 0.5f)
 120: 	{
 121: 		// A round-by-round gun counts its reserve in rounds: the same total as the magazines would have held.
 122: 		Full.SpareMagazines *= Full.InMagazine;
 123: 	}
 124: 	return Full;
 125: }

 127: bool UGBEquipmentComponent::GetWeaponAmmo(FName WeaponId, FGBAmmoState& OutAmmo) const
 128: {
 129: 	if (!OwnsWeapon(WeaponId))
 130: 	{
 230: 	}
 231: 
 232: 	USceneComponent* Anchor = Player->GetHeldItemAnchor() ? Player->GetHeldItemAnchor() : Player->GetRootComponent();
 233: 	Item->AttachToComponent(Anchor, FAttachmentTransformRules::SnapToTargetNotIncludingScale);

 239: 
 240: void UGBEquipmentComponent::StoreItemState(AGBHeldItem* Item)
 241: {
 242: 	if (!IsValid(Item))
 243: 	{
 244: 		return;
 245: 	}
 246: 	Item->StopPrimary();
 247: 	Item->StopSecondary();
 248: 	Item->OnUnequipped(); // a gun cancels its reload here
 249: 
 250: 	const FName WeaponId = Item->GetWeaponId();
 251: 	StoredElements.Add(WeaponId, Item->GetElement());
 252: 	if (Item->UsesAmmo())
 253: 	{
 254: 		StoredAmmo.Add(WeaponId, Item->GetAmmo());
 255: 	}
 256: }
 257: 
 258: void UGBEquipmentComponent::RestoreItemState(AGBHeldItem* Item)
 259: {
 260: 	if (!IsValid(Item))
 261: 	{
 262: 		return;
 263: 	}
 264: 	const FName WeaponId = Item->GetWeaponId();
 265: 	if (const EGBElement* Element = StoredElements.Find(WeaponId))
 266: 	{
 267: 		Item->SetElement(*Element);
 268: 	}
 269: 	if (const FGBAmmoState* Ammo = StoredAmmo.Find(WeaponId))
 270: 	{
 271: 		Item->SetAmmo(*Ammo); // a gun never held has no entry and stays full from SpawnItem
 272: 	}
 273: 	// While in a slot, the actor is the only source of truth.
 274: 	StoredElements.Remove(WeaponId);
 275: 	StoredAmmo.Remove(WeaponId);
 276: }
 277: 
 278: // ---- Giving and equipping ----
 279: 
 280: AGBHeldItem* UGBEquipmentComponent::GiveWeapon(FName WeaponId, bool bAutoSwitch, bool /*bDropOld*/)
 281: {
 282: 	EGBHeldSlot NewSlot = EGBHeldSlot::Staff;
 283: 	if (!FindWeaponSlot(WeaponId, NewSlot))
 284: 	{
 285: 		UE_LOG(LogGB, Warning, TEXT("GiveWeapon: unknown weapon '%s'"), *WeaponId.ToString());
 286: 		return nullptr;
 287: 	}
 288: 
 289: 	if (OwnsWeapon(WeaponId))
 290: 	{
 291: 		return FindEquippedItem(WeaponId);
 292: 	}
 293: 
 294: 	// Owner's rule (2026-11-12): nothing is dropped and the weapon in hand is never replaced.
 295: 	if (HasWeaponInSlot(NewSlot))
 296: 	{
 297: 		OwnedWeapons.Add(WeaponId);
 298: 		if (NewSlot == EGBHeldSlot::Gun)
 299: 		{
 300: 			StoredAmmo.Add(WeaponId, ComputeFullAmmo(WeaponId));
 301: 		}
 302: 		OnEquipmentChanged.Broadcast();
 303: 		return nullptr;
 304: 	}
 305: 
 306: 	const bool bHandsEmpty = GetActiveItem() == nullptr;
 307: 	AGBHeldItem* NewItem = SpawnItem(WeaponId);
 308: 	if (!NewItem)
 309: 	{
 310: 		return nullptr;
 311: 	}
 312: 	OwnedWeapons.Add(WeaponId);
 313: 	Items.Add(NewSlot, NewItem);
 314: 
 315: 	if (bHandsEmpty)
 316: 	{
 317: 		EquipSlot(NewSlot);
 318: 	}
 319: 	else if (!bAutoSwitch || !SwitchTo(NewSlot))
 320: 	{
 321: 		OnEquipmentChanged.Broadcast();
 322: 	}
 323: 	return NewItem;
 324: }
 325: 
 326: bool UGBEquipmentComponent::EquipOwnedWeapon(FName WeaponId)
 327: {
 328: 	if (bSwitchLocked || !OwnsWeapon(WeaponId) || IsWeaponEquipped(WeaponId))
 329: 	{
 330: 		return false;
 430: bool UGBEquipmentComponent::SwitchTo(EGBHeldSlot Slot)
 431: {
 432: 	if (bSwitchLocked || !HasWeaponInSlot(Slot))
 433: 	{
 434: 		return false;
 435: 	}
 436: 	if (Slot == ActiveSlot && GetActiveItem() != nullptr)
 437: 	{
 438: 		return true;
 439: 	}
 440: 	EquipSlot(Slot);
 441: 	return true;
 442: }
 443: 
 444: void UGBEquipmentComponent::CycleSlot(int32 Direction)
 445: {
 446: 	if (bSwitchLocked)
 447: 	{
 448: 		return;
 449: 	}
 450: 	const int32 Count = static_cast<int32>(UE_ARRAY_COUNT(EquipSlotOrder));
 451: 	const int32 Step = Direction >= 0 ? 1 : -1;
 452: 	const int32 Start = EquipSlotIndex(ActiveSlot);
 453: 	for (int32 Offset = 1; Offset < Count; ++Offset)
 454: 	{
 455: 		const int32 Index = ((Start + Step * Offset) % Count + Count) % Count;
 456: 		if (HasWeaponInSlot(EquipSlotOrder[Index]))
 457: 		{
 458: 			SwitchTo(EquipSlotOrder[Index]);
 459: 			return;
 460: 		}
 461: 	}
 462: }
 463: 
 464: // ---- Ammo ----
 465: 
 466: void UGBEquipmentComponent::RefillAllAmmo()
 467: {
 468: 	for (const TPair<EGBHeldSlot, TObjectPtr<AGBHeldItem>>& Pair : Items)
 469: 	{
 470: 		if (AGBHeldItem* Item = Pair.Value.Get(); IsValid(Item))
 471: 		{
 472: 			Item->RefillAmmo();
 473: 		}
 474: 	}
 475: 	// Era travel restores everything (GDD 8), stored guns included.
 476: 	for (const FName& WeaponId : OwnedWeapons)
 477: 	{
 478: 		EGBHeldSlot Slot = EGBHeldSlot::Staff;
 479: 		if (!IsWeaponEquipped(WeaponId) && FindWeaponSlot(WeaponId, Slot) && Slot == EGBHeldSlot::Gun)
 480: 		{
 481: 			StoredAmmo.Add(WeaponId, ComputeFullAmmo(WeaponId));
 482: 		}
 483: 	}
 484: 	OnEquipmentChanged.Broadcast();
 485: }
 486: 
 487: int32 UGBEquipmentComponent::AddSpareMagazines(int32 Count)
 488: {
 489: 	AGBHeldItem* Gun = GetItemInSlot(EGBHeldSlot::Gun);
 490: 	return Gun ? Gun->AddSpareMagazines(Count) : 0;
 570: 		}
 571: 	}
 572: 	for (const TPair<FName, EGBElement>& Pair : StoredElements)
 573: 	{
 574: 		if (!Snapshot.WeaponElements.Contains(Pair.Key))
 575: 		{
 576: 			Snapshot.WeaponElements.Add(Pair.Key, Pair.Value);
 577: 		}
 578: 	}
 579: 	Snapshot.ActiveSlot = ActiveSlot;
 580: }
 581: 
 582: void UGBEquipmentComponent::LoadFromSnapshot(const FGBPlayerSnapshot& Snapshot)
 583: {
 584: 	DestroyAllItems();
 585: 	OwnedWeapons.Reset();
 586: 	StoredAmmo.Reset();
 587: 	StoredElements.Reset();
 588: 
 589: 	for (const FName& WeaponId : Snapshot.Weapons)
 590: 	{
 591: 		EGBHeldSlot Slot = EGBHeldSlot::Staff;
 592: 		if (FindWeaponSlot(WeaponId, Slot))
 593: 		{
 594: 			OwnedWeapons.AddUnique(WeaponId);
 595: 		}

## The smoke run
`gb.Smoke` has a block per gun in `Core/GBDevCommands.cpp` (search for `"rifle:"`). Add a shotgun block after the rifle's, in the same style, with screenshots `15a` onwards. Checks, each able to fail:
- the shotgun is in hand with its real model: 6 meshes (body, trigger, shell, weak-point light, ammo display, pump), not placeholders; the display shows `6`; screenshot `15a_shotgun_hip`;
- one shot: the display shows `5`, the kick and the muzzle flash happen, and the pump leaves rest and comes back to rest before the next shot is allowed; with the pump pinned at the back of its travel, screenshot `15b_shotgun_pump`;
- weak-point flash on a head hit, 0 on a body hit; the energy bar at half an enchant; screenshot `15c_shotgun_enchanted`;
- reload from 3 shells with 24 in reserve: after one cycle 4 and 23, the display follows; it ends at 6 and 21 after about half of `ReloadTime`; with a shell's cycle pinned mid-way (the shell visible and away from rest, the root away from rest), screenshot `15d_shotgun_reload_shell`;
- fire pressed during a reload from 2 shells: the reload stops, the shot is fired, and the counts add up (nothing lost, nothing created);
- a reload cancelled by switching to the staff and back keeps the shells already loaded;
- after all of it the pump and the shell are at rest and the root is at rest.
Every other smoke line must stay as it is.

- `powershell -NoProfile -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File Tools/Jam/Test-Jam.ps1` with the anchored filter of the weapons tests (read the script's header for its parameters; filters look like `^Glyphbound.Weapons.`): all green.

+// ---- Round-by-round reload stepping (pure) ----
+
+void FGBPerRoundReload::Begin(int32 Rounds, int32 Reserve, int32 Capacity, float TotalSeconds)
+{
+	CycleSeconds = Capacity > 0 ? FMath::Max(0.f, TotalSeconds) / Capacity : 0.f;
+	CycleElapsed = 0.f;
+	Moved = 0;
+	Planned = FMath::Max(0, FMath::Min(Capacity - Rounds, Reserve));
+	bRunning = Planned > 0;
+}
+
+int32 FGBPerRoundReload::Advance(float DeltaSeconds, int32& Rounds, int32& Reserve, int32 Capacity)
+{
+	if (!bRunning)
+	{
+		return 0;
+	}
+	int32 MovedNow = 0;
+	CycleElapsed += FMath::Max(0.f, DeltaSeconds);
+	// The small tolerance keeps six cycles of 1/3 s from falling a float rounding error short of 2 s.
+	while (bRunning && CycleElapsed + 0.0001f >= CycleSeconds)
+	{
+		CycleElapsed = FMath::Max(0.f, CycleElapsed - CycleSeconds);
+		if (Rounds < Capacity && Reserve > 0)
+		{
+			++Rounds;
+			--Reserve;
+			++Moved;
+			++MovedNow;
+		}
+		if (Rounds >= Capacity || Reserve <= 0)
+		{
+			bRunning = false;
+			CycleElapsed = 0.f;
+		}
+	}
+	return MovedNow;
+}
+
+void FGBPerRoundReload::Stop()
+{
+	bRunning = false;
+	CycleElapsed = 0.f;
+}
+
+float FGBPerRoundReload::Progress() const
+{
+	if (Planned <= 0)
+	{
+		return 1.f;
+	}
+	return FMath::Clamp((Moved + CycleProgress()) / Planned, 0.f, 1.f);
+}
+
+float FGBPerRoundReload::CycleProgress() const
+{
+	return (bRunning && CycleSeconds > KINDA_SMALL_NUMBER) ? FMath::Clamp(CycleElapsed / CycleSeconds, 0.f, 1.f) : 0.f;
+}

+
+void AGBGun::ResetPump()
+{
+	PumpElapsed = -1.f;
+	PumpPreview = -1.f;
+	if (bViewModel)
+	{
+		ApplyPump();
+	}
+}
+
+void AGBGun::ApplyPump()
+{
+	float Amount = 0.f;
+	if (ModelArt && ModelArt->bPump)
+	{
+		if (PumpPreview >= 0.f)
+		{
+			Amount = PumpPreview;
+		}
+		else if (PumpElapsed >= 0.f)
+		{
+			// Shares of the current FireInterval: still, back, fully back, home, still.
+			const float T = PumpElapsed / FMath::Max(0.05f, GetStat(GBStat::FireInterval, 0.5f));
+			if (T >= PumpStartShare && T < PumpBackShare)
+			{
+				Amount = Smoothstep01(Share(T, PumpStartShare, PumpBackShare));
+			}
+			else if (T >= PumpBackShare && T < PumpHomeShare)
+			{
+				Amount = 1.f - Smoothstep01(Share(T, PumpBackShare, PumpHomeShare));
+			}
+		}
+	}
+	CurrentPumpAmount = Amount;
+	if (VMPump && ModelArt)
+	{
+		VMPump->SetRelativeLocation(ModelArt->PumpRest + ModelArt->PumpSlide * Amount);
+	}
+}
+
 float AGBGun::GetReloadAnimProgress() const
 {
 	if (ReloadPreview >= 0.f)
 	{
 		return ReloadPreview;
 	}
-	return bReloading ? GetReloadProgress() : -1.f;
+	if (!bReloading)
+	{
+		return -1.f;
+	}
+	return ReloadsPerRound() ? PerRound.CycleProgress() : GetReloadProgress();
+}
+
+FGBReloadLook AGBGun::ComputeReloadLook() const
+{
+	FGBReloadLook Look;
+	const float Progress = GetReloadAnimProgress();
+	if (Progress < 0.f)
+	{
+		return Look;
+	}
+	Look.bActive = true;
+	if (!ReloadsPerRound())
+	{
+		// Magazine reload: slide out along MagazineOut, (fall), (rise), slide in; the slide share is 1 while the magazine is away.
+		Look.Pose = GBReloadAnim::PoseAmount(Progress);
+		Look.Dip = ReloadDip(Progress);
+		Look.Bump = ReloadBump(Progress);
+		Look.Drop = ReloadDrop(Progress);
+		Look.Slide = 1.f;
+		if (Progress < ReloadSlideOutEnd)
+		{
+			Look.Slide = Smoothstep01(Share(Progress, ReloadTiltEnd, ReloadSlideOutEnd));
+		}
+		else if (Progress >= ReloadRiseEnd)
+		{
+			Look.Slide = 1.f - FMath::Pow(Share(Progress, ReloadRiseEnd, ReloadInEnd), ReloadSlideInEase);
+		}
+		Look.bShowMag = Progress < ReloadOutEnd || Progress >= ReloadEmptyEnd;
+		return Look;
+	}
+	// One round of a round-by-round reload: the "fresh magazine" beats of the magazine guns, once per round.
+	// The pose eases in during the first round only (a pinned preview holds it fully); the last ease-out is the blend after the end.
+	const bool bFirstRound = ReloadPreview < 0.f && PerRound.Moved == 0;
+	Look.Pose = bFirstRound ? Smoothstep01(Progress / PerRoundPoseInShare) : 1.f;
+	const float Beat = FMath::Lerp(ReloadEmptyEnd, ReloadSeatEnd, Progress);
+	Look.Bump = ReloadBump(Beat);
+	Look.Drop = ReloadDrop(Beat);
+	Look.Slide = Beat < ReloadRiseEnd ? 1.f : 1.f - FMath::Pow(Share(Beat, ReloadRiseEnd, ReloadInEnd), ReloadSlideInEase);
+	Look.bShowMag = Progress < 0.98f; // seated: inside the port, the next round starts below the view
+	return Look;
+}
+
+GBReloadAnim::FHandBeat AGBGun::GetReloadHandBeat() const
+{
+	GBReloadAnim::FHandBeat Beat;
+	if (!bViewModel)
+	{
+		return Beat;
+	}
+	const FGBReloadLook Look = ComputeReloadLook();
+	if (Look.bActive)
+	{
+		Beat.Reach = Look.Pose;
+		Beat.Drop = Look.Drop;
+		Beat.Push = Look.Bump;
+	}
+	else if (ReloadsPerRound())
+	{
+		Beat.Reach = CurrentPoseAmount;
+	}
+	return Beat;
+}
+
+float AGBGun::GetReloadPoseAmount() const
+{
+	if (!bViewModel)
+	{
+		return 0.f;
+	}
+	const FGBReloadLook Look = ComputeReloadLook();
+	return Look.bActive ? Look.Pose : (ReloadsPerRound() ? CurrentPoseAmount : 0.f);
 }
